   26:  *
   27:  * posicoesFromOptionsMode:
   28:  * - "ignore" (default): IGNORA options.posicoes (posição é domínio fixo)

   30:  *
   31:  * Extras (freeze-safe):

   33:  * - onBlocked: callback quando usuário tenta interagir enquanto bloqueado
   34:  */
   35: 
   36: function normalizeValue(v, fallback = "Todos") {
   37:   const s = String(v ?? "").trim();
   38:   return s ? s : fallback;
   39: }
   40: 
   41: function sortPTBR(a, b) {
   42:   return String(a).localeCompare(String(b), "pt-BR", { sensitivity: "base" });
   43: }
   44: 
   45: function normalizeLoteriaInput(v) {
   46:   const raw = String(v ?? "").trim();
   47:   if (!raw) return "PT_RIO";
   48: 
   49:   const key = raw
   50:     .toLowerCase()
   51:     .normalize("NFD")
   52:     .replace(/[\u0300-\u036f]/g, "")
   53:     .replace(/\s+/g, " ")
   54:     .trim();
   55: 
   56:   if (key === "federal" || key === "fed" || key === "br" || key === "brasil")
   57:     return "FEDERAL";
   58:   if (key === "rj" || key === "rio" || key === "pt_rio" || key === "pt-rio")
   59:     return "PT_RIO";
   60: 
   61:   const out = key
   62:     .toUpperCase()
   63:     .replace(/[^A-Z0-9]+/g, "_")
   64:     .replace(/^_+|_+$/g, "");
   65: 
   66:   return out || "PT_RIO";
   67: }
   68: 
   69: /** Normaliza posição para "1º..7º" ou "Todos" */
   70: function normalizePosicaoInput(v) {
   71:   const s = String(v ?? "").trim();
   72:   if (!s) return "Todos";
   73:   if (s.toLowerCase() === "todos") return "Todos";
   74: 
   75:   const mNum = s.match(/^(\d{1,2})$/);
   76:   if (mNum) return `${Number(mNum[1])}º`;
   77: 
   78:   const mOrd = s.match(/^(\d{1,2})\s*º$/);
   79:   if (mOrd) return `${Number(mOrd[1])}º`;
   80: 
   81:   return s;
   82: }
   83:

  133: /** Garante que o value exista na lista */
  134: function ensureValueInList({
  135:   value,
  136:   list,
  137:   forceValue = null,
  138:   fallback = "Todos",
  139:   normalizeFn = null,
  140: }) {
  141:   const base = forceValue ?? value;
  142:   const v0 = normalizeValue(base, fallback);
  143:   const v = typeof normalizeFn === "function" ? normalizeFn(v0) : v0;
  144: 
  145:   const arr = Array.isArray(list) ? list : [];
  146:   if (!arr.length) return v;
  147: 
  148:   const isObjList = typeof arr[0] === "object" && arr[0] && "value" in arr[0];
  149: 
  150:   const norm = (x) => {
  151:     const s = normalizeValue(x, "");
  152:     return typeof normalizeFn === "function" ? normalizeFn(s) : s;
  153:   };
  154: 
  155:   if (isObjList) {
  156:     const values = new Set(arr.map((x) => norm(x?.value)));
  157:     if (values.has(v)) return v;
  158:     if (values.has("Todos")) return "Todos";
  159:     const first = norm(arr[0]?.value);
  160:     return first || fallback;
  161:   }
  162:

  164:   if (values.has(v)) return v;
  165:   if (values.has("Todos")) return "Todos";
  166: 
  167:   const first = norm(arr[0]);
  168:   return first || fallback;
  169: }

  211:   const loteria = useMemo(
  212:     () => normalizeLoteriaInput(filters?.loteria),
  213:     [filters?.loteria]
  214:   );
  215:   const defaultOptions = useMemo(() => {
  216:     const loteriasDefault = [
  217:       { label: "RJ", value: "PT_RIO" },
  218:       { label: "FEDERAL", value: "FEDERAL" },
  219:       { label: "LOOK", value: "LOOK" },
  220:       { label: "NACIONAL", value: "NACIONAL" },
  221:     ];
  222: 
  223:     const mesesDefault = [
  224:       "Todos",
  225:       "Janeiro",
  226:       "Fevereiro",
  227:       "Março",
  228:       "Abril",
  229:       "Maio",
  230:       "Junho",
  231:       "Julho",
  232:       "Agosto",
  233:       "Setembro",
  234:       "Outubro",
  235:       "Novembro",
  236:       "Dezembro",
  237:     ];
  238: 
  239:     const diasSemanaDefault = [
  240:       { label: "Todos", value: "Todos" },
  241:       { label: "Domingo", value: "Dom" },
  242:       { label: "Segunda-Feira", value: "Seg" },
  243:       { label: "Terça-Feira", value: "Ter" },
  244:       { label: "Quarta-Feira", value: "Qua" },
  245:       { label: "Quinta-Feira", value: "Qui" },
  246:       { label: "Sexta-Feira", value: "Sex" },
  247:       { label: "Sábado", value: "Sáb" },
  248:     ];
  249: 
  250:     // ✅ PT_RIO (domínio fixo)
  251:     const horariosRJ = [
  252:       { label: "Todos", value: "Todos" },
  253:       { label: "09h", value: "09h" },
  254:       { label: "11h", value: "11h" },
  255:       { label: "14h", value: "14h" },
  256:       { label: "16h", value: "16h" },
  257:       { label: "18h", value: "18h" },
  258:       { label: "21h", value: "21h" },
  259:     ];
  260: 
  261:     // ✅ FEDERAL
  262:     const horariosFED = [
  263:       { label: "Todos", value: "Todos" },
  264:       { label: "19h", value: "19h" },
  265:       { label: "20h", value: "20h" },
  266:     ];
  267: 
  268:     // ✅ LOOK
  269:     const horariosLOOK = [
  270:       { label: "Todos", value: "Todos" },
  271:       { label: "07h", value: "07h" },
  272:       { label: "09h", value: "09h" },
  273:       { label: "11h", value: "11h" },
  274:       { label: "14h", value: "14h" },
  275:       { label: "16h", value: "16h" },
  276:       { label: "18h", value: "18h" },
  277:       { label: "21h", value: "21h" },
  278:       { label: "23h", value: "23h" },
  279:     ];
  280: 
  281:     // ✅ NACIONAL
  282:     // O label mantém o horário oficial exibido ao usuário.
  283:     // O value usa o bucket da hora real persistida no Firestore
  284:     // (ex.: 14:50 pertence ao bucket 14h, mas é exibido como 15h).
  285:     const horariosNACIONAL = [
  286:       { label: "Todos", value: "Todos" },
  287:       { label: "02h", value: "01h" },
  288:       { label: "08h", value: "07h" },
  289:       { label: "10h", value: "09h" },
  290:       { label: "12h", value: "11h" },
  291:       { label: "15h", value: "14h" },
  292:       { label: "17h", value: "16h" },
  293:       { label: "21h", value: "20h" },
  294:       { label: "23h", value: "22h" },
  295:     ];
  296: 
  297:     const posicoesDefaultFull = [
  298:       { label: "Todos", value: "Todos" },
  299:       { label: "1º", value: "1º" },
  300:       { label: "2º", value: "2º" },
  301:       { label: "3º", value: "3º" },
  302:       { label: "4º", value: "4º" },
  303:       { label: "5º", value: "5º" },
  304:       { label: "6º", value: "6º" },
  305:       { label: "7º", value: "7º" },
  306:     ];
  307: 
  308:     const posicoesDefaultV1 = [{ label: "1º", value: "1º" }];
  309: 
  310:     const normalizeLabelValueList = (arr, fallback) => {
  311:       if (!Array.isArray(arr) || !arr.length) return fallback;
  312:       const first = arr[0];
  313: 
  314:       if (
  315:         typeof first === "object" &&
  316:         first &&
  317:         "label" in first &&
  318:         "value" in first
  319:       ) {
  320:         return arr
  321:           .map((x) => ({
  322:             label: normalizeValue(x?.label, "Todos"),
  323:             value: normalizeValue(x?.value, "Todos"),
  324:           }))
  325:           .filter((x) => x.label && x.value);
  326:       }
  327: 
  328:       return arr
  329:         .map((x) => {
  330:           const v = normalizeValue(x, "");
  331:           return v ? { label: v, value: v } : null;
  332:         })
  333:         .filter(Boolean);
  334:     };
  335: 
  336:     // aceita string OU {value,label} em options.animais
  337:     const animais =
  338:       Array.isArray(options.animais) && options.animais.length
  339:         ? (() => {
  340:             const cleaned = options.animais
  341:               .map((x) => {
  342:                 if (x && typeof x === "object")
  343:                   return String(x.value ?? x.label ?? "").trim();
  344:                 return String(x ?? "").trim();
  345:               })
  346:               .filter(Boolean)
  347:               .filter((x) => x.toLowerCase() !== "todos");
  348: 
  349:             const uniq = Array.from(new Set(cleaned));
  350:             uniq.sort(sortPTBR);
  351:             return ["Todos", ...uniq];
  352:           })()
  353:         : ["Todos"];
  354: 
  355:     // aceita string OU {value,label} em options.diasMes + dedup
  356:     const diasMes =
  357:       Array.isArray(options.diasMes) && options.diasMes.length
  358:         ? (() => {
  359:             const cleaned = options.diasMes
  360:               .map((x) => {
  361:                 if (x && typeof x === "object")
  362:                   return String(x.value ?? x.label ?? "").trim();
  363:                 return String(x ?? "").trim();
  364:               })
  365:               .filter(Boolean)
  366:               .filter((x) => x.toLowerCase() !== "todos");
  367:             const uniq = Array.from(new Set(cleaned));
  368:             return ["Todos", ...uniq];
  369:           })()
  370:         : ["Todos", ...Array.from({ length: 31 }, (_, i) => String(i + 1))];
  371: 
  372:     const diasSemanaFromParent =
  373:       Array.isArray(options.diasSemana) && options.diasSemana.length
  374:         ? normalizeLabelValueList(options.diasSemana, diasSemanaDefault)
  375:         : diasSemanaDefault;
  376: 
  377:     // ✅ Cada loteria mantém seu próprio domínio de horários.
  378:     const horariosPorLoteria = {
  379:       PT_RIO: horariosRJ,
  380:       FEDERAL: horariosFED,
  381:       LOOK: horariosLOOK,
  382:       NACIONAL: horariosNACIONAL,
  383:     };
  384: 
  385:     const horarios = horariosPorLoteria[loteria] || horariosRJ;
  386: 
  387:     const posicoesFromParent =
  388:       posicoesFromOptionsMode === "respect" &&
  389:       Array.isArray(options.posicoes) &&
  390:       options.posicoes.length
  391:         ? normalizeLabelValueList(options.posicoes, posicoesDefaultFull)
  392:         : null;
  393: 
  394:     const posicoes =
  395:       posicoesFromParent ||
  396:       (posicaoMode === "v1" ? posicoesDefaultV1 : posicoesDefaultFull);
  397: 
  398:     const meses =
  399:       Array.isArray(options.meses) && options.meses.length
  400:         ? (() => {
  401:             const cleaned = options.meses
  402:               .map((x) => {
  403:                 if (x && typeof x === "object")
  404:                   return String(x.value ?? x.label ?? "").trim();
  405:                 return String(x ?? "").trim();
  406:               })
  407:               .filter(Boolean)
  408:               .filter((x) => x.toLowerCase() !== "todos");
  409:             const uniq = Array.from(new Set(cleaned));
  410:             return ["Todos", ...uniq];
  411:           })()
  412:         : mesesDefault;
  413: 
  414:     return {
  415:       loterias: loteriasDefault,
  416:       meses,
  417:       diasMes,
  418:       diasSemana: diasSemanaFromParent,
  419:       horarios,
  420:       animais,
  421:       posicoes,
  422:     };
  423:   }, [options, posicaoMode, posicoesFromOptionsMode, loteria]);
  424: 
  425:   const ui = {
  426:     bar: {
  427:       border: `1px solid ${PP.strokeStrong}`,
  428:       borderRadius: PP.rCard,
  429:       background: `linear-gradient(180deg, ${PP.surface}, ${PP.surface2})`,
  430:       padding: 12,
  431:       boxShadow: PP.shadowGlow,
  432:       overflow: "hidden",
  433:       minWidth: 0,
  434:     },
  435:     grid: {
  436:       display: "grid",
  437:       gridTemplateColumns: "repeat(12, minmax(0, 1fr))",
  438:       gap: 10,
  439:       alignItems: "end",
  440:       minWidth: 0,
  441:     },
  442:     spanLoteria: { gridColumn: "span 2", minWidth: 0 },
  443:     spanMes: { gridColumn: "span 2", minWidth: 0 },
  444:     spanDiaMes: { gridColumn: "span 2", minWidth: 0 },
  445:     spanDiaSemana: { gridColumn: "span 3", minWidth: 0 },
  446:     spanHorario: { gridColumn: "span 1", minWidth: 0 },
  447:     spanAnimal: { gridColumn: "span 1", minWidth: 0 },
  448:     spanPosicao: { gridColumn: "span 1", minWidth: 0 },
  449:     item: { display: "grid", gap: 7, minWidth: 0 },
  450:     label: {
  451:       fontWeight: 700,
  452:       letterSpacing: 0.12,
  453:       fontSize: "clamp(13px, 1.0vw, 15px)",
  454:       color: PP.text,
  455:       opacity: 0.9,
  456:       lineHeight: 1.1,
  457:       whiteSpace: "nowrap",
  458:       overflow: "hidden",
  459:       textOverflow: "ellipsis",
  460:       minWidth: 0,
  461:     },
  462:     selectWrap: { position: "relative", width: "100%", minWidth: 0 },
  463:     select: {
  464:       height: PP.inputH,
  465:       borderRadius: 12,
  466:       border: `1px solid ${PP.stroke}`,
  467:       background: "rgba(0,0,0,0.42)",
  468:       color: PP.text,
  469:       padding: "0 44px 0 12px",
  470:       outline: "none",
  471:       width: "100%",
  472:       fontWeight: 650,
  473:       fontSize: "clamp(13px, 1.0vw, 15px)",
  474:       letterSpacing: 0.1,
  475:       appearance: "none",
  476:       WebkitAppearance: "none",
  477:       MozAppearance: "none",
  478:       boxShadow: "inset 0 0 0 1px rgba(0,0,0,0.28)",
  479:       cursor: "pointer",
  480:       minWidth: 0,
  481:       transition:
  482:         "border-color 160ms ease, box-shadow 160ms ease, transform 160ms ease",
  483:       whiteSpace: "nowrap",
  484:       textOverflow: "clip",
  485:     },
  486:     selectDisabled: {
  487:       opacity: 0.62,
  488:       cursor: "not-allowed",
  489:       filter: "grayscale(18%)",
  490:     },
  491:     caret: {
  492:       position: "absolute",
  493:       right: 14,
  494:       top: "50%",
  495:       transform: "translateY(-50%)",
  496:       width: 0,
  497:       height: 0,
  498:       borderLeft: "7px solid transparent",
  499:       borderRight: "7px solid transparent",
  500:       borderTop: `8px solid ${PP.text2}`,

  502:       opacity: 0.9,
  503:     },
  504:     styleTag: `
  505:       .pp_filters_grid * { box-sizing: border-box; }

  509:         .pp_f_loteria{ grid-column: span 2 !important; }

  513:         .pp_f_horario{ grid-column: span 2 !important; }
  514:         .pp_f_animal{ grid-column: span 2 !important; }
  515:         .pp_f_posicao{ grid-column: span 2 !important; }
  516:       }

  519:         .pp_f_loteria,

  531:         border-color: rgba(200,178,90,0.55) !important;

  533:       }

  536:         border-color: rgba(200,178,90,0.20);

  551:         color: #0B0B0C !important;

  555:         background-color: ${PP.selGoldB} !important;
  556:         color: #0B0B0C !important;
  557:       }
  558: 
  559:       .pp_filters_grid select option:checked{
  560:         background: linear-gradient(180deg, ${PP.selGoldA}, ${PP.selGoldB}) !important;
  561:       }
  562: 
  563:       .pp_filters_grid select:focus-visible{
  564:         outline: none;
  565:       }
  566:     `,
  567:   };
  568: 
  569:   const lockPosicaoV1 = posicaoMode === "v1";
  570:   const posicaoForcedValue = lockPosicaoV1 ? "1º" : null;
  571: 
  572:   const handleChange = useCallback(
  573:     (name, next) => {
  574:       if (disabledAll) {
  575:         if (typeof onBlocked === "function") onBlocked(name);
  576:         return;
  577:       }
  578: 
  579:       // ✅ Troca loteria: normaliza e reseta filtros sensíveis SEMPRE
  580:       if (name === "loteria") {
  581:         const nextLot = normalizeLoteriaInput(next);
  582:         onChange("loteria", nextLot);
  583: 
  584:         // reset para evitar “estatística inválida” por estado anterior
  585:         onChange("horario", "Todos");
  586:         onChange("animal", "Todos");
  587:         onChange("posicao", "Todos");
  588: 
  589:         return;
  590:       }
  591: 
  592:       if (lockPosicaoV1 && name === "posicao") {
  593:         onChange("posicao", "1º");
  594:         return;
  595:       }
  596: 
  597:       if (name === "posicao") {
  598:         onChange(name, normalizePosicaoInput(next));
  599:         return;
  600:       }
  601: 
  602:       if (name === "diaSemana") {
  603:         onChange(name, normalizeDiaSemanaInput(next));
  604:         return;
  605:       }
  606: 
  607:       onChange(name, next);
  608:     },
  609:     [disabledAll, onBlocked, lockPosicaoV1, onChange]
  610:   );
  611: 
  612:   const Item = ({
  613:     label,
  614:     value,
  615:     name,
  616:     list,
  617:     wrapClassName,
  618:     wrapStyle,
  619:     disabled = false,
  620:     forceValue = null,
  621:   }) => {
  622:     const normalizeFn =
  623:       name === "posicao"
  624:         ? normalizePosicaoInput
  625:         : name === "diaSemana"
  626:         ? normalizeDiaSemanaInput
  627:         : name === "loteria"
  628:         ? normalizeLoteriaInput
  629:         : null;
  630: 
  631:     const normalizedValue = ensureValueInList({
  632:       value,
  633:       list,
  634:       forceValue,
  635:       fallback: name === "loteria" ? "PT_RIO" : "Todos",
  636:       normalizeFn,
  637:     });
  638: 
  639:     const isObjList =
  640:       Array.isArray(list) && list.length && typeof list[0] === "object" && list[0];
  641: 
  642:     const finalDisabled = !!disabledAll || !!disabled;
  643: 
  644:     const block = (e) => {
  645:       if (!finalDisabled) return;
  646:       try {
  647:         e?.preventDefault?.();
  648:         e?.stopPropagation?.();
  649:       } catch {}
  650:       if (typeof onBlocked === "function") onBlocked(name);
  651:     };
  652: 
  653:     return (
  654:       <div className={wrapClassName} style={{ ...wrapStyle, minWidth: 0 }}>
  655:         <div style={ui.item}>
  656:           <div style={ui.label} title={label}>
  657:             {label}
  658:           </div>
  659: 
  660:           <div style={ui.selectWrap}>
  661:             <select
  662:               value={normalizedValue}
  663:               aria-disabled={finalDisabled ? "true" : "false"}
  664:               tabIndex={finalDisabled ? -1 : 0}   // ✅ FIX: evita foco via TAB quando bloqueado
  665:               onWheelCapture={block}              // ✅ FIX: bloqueia troca por roda do mouse
  666:               onMouseDownCapture={block}
  667:               onClickCapture={block}
  668:               onKeyDownCapture={(e) => {
  669:                 if (!finalDisabled) return;
  670:                 block(e);
  671:               }}
  672:               onChange={(e) => handleChange(name, e.target.value)}
  673:               style={{
  674:                 ...ui.select,
  675:                 ...(finalDisabled ? ui.selectDisabled : null),
  676:               }}
  677:             >
  678:               {isObjList
  679:                 ? list.map((opt, idx) => (
  680:                     <option key={`${name}_${String(opt?.value)}_${idx}`} value={opt?.value}>
  681:                       {opt?.label}
  682:                     </option>
  683:                   ))
  684:                 : (list || ["Todos"]).map((opt, idx) => (
  685:                     <option key={`${name}_${String(opt)}_${idx}`} value={opt}>
  686:                       {opt}
  687:                     </option>
  688:                   ))}
  689:             </select>
  690: 
  691:             <span aria-hidden="true" style={ui.caret} />
  692:           </div>
  693:         </div>
  694:       </div>
  695:     );
  696:   };
  697: 
  698:   // FEDERAL NÃO trava horário — só troca a lista (Todos/19h/20h)
  699:   const horarioForcedValue = null;
  700:   const horarioDisabled = false;
  701: 
  702:   return (
  703:     <div style={ui.bar}>
  704:       <style>{ui.styleTag}</style>
  705: 
  706:       <div className="pp_filters_grid" style={ui.grid}>
  707:         <Item
  708:           label="Loteria"
  709:           name="loteria"
  710:           value={loteria}
  711:           list={defaultOptions.loterias}
  712:           wrapClassName="pp_f_loteria"
  713:           wrapStyle={ui.spanLoteria}
  714:         />
  715: 
  716:         <Item
  717:           label="Mês"
  718:           name="mes"
  719:           value={filters.mes}
  720:           list={defaultOptions.meses}
  721:           wrapClassName="pp_f_mes"
  722:           wrapStyle={ui.spanMes}
  723:         />
  724: 
  725:         <Item
  726:           label="Dia do Mês"
  727:           name="diaMes"
  728:           value={filters.diaMes}
  729:           list={defaultOptions.diasMes}
  730:           wrapClassName="pp_f_diaMes"
  731:           wrapStyle={ui.spanDiaMes}
  732:         />
  733: 
  734:         <Item
  735:           label="Dia da Semana"
  736:           name="diaSemana"
  737:           value={filters.diaSemana}
  738:           list={defaultOptions.diasSemana}
  739:           wrapClassName="pp_f_diaSemana"
  740:           wrapStyle={ui.spanDiaSemana}
  741:         />
  742: 
  743:         <Item
  744:           label="Horário"
  745:           name="horario"
  746:           value={filters.horario}
  747:           list={defaultOptions.horarios}
  748:           wrapClassName="pp_f_horario"
  749:           wrapStyle={ui.spanHorario}
  750:           disabled={horarioDisabled}
  751:           forceValue={horarioForcedValue}
  752:         />
  753: 
  754:         <Item
  755:           label="Animal"
  756:           name="animal"
  757:           value={filters.animal}
  758:           list={defaultOptions.animais}
  759:           wrapClassName="pp_f_animal"
  760:           wrapStyle={ui.spanAnimal}
  761:         />
  762: 
  763:         <Item
  764:           label="Posição"
  765:           name="posicao"
  766:           value={filters.posicao}
  767:           list={defaultOptions.posicoes}
  768:           wrapClassName="pp_f_posicao"
  769:           wrapStyle={ui.spanPosicao}
  770:           disabled={lockPosicaoV1}
  771:           forceValue={posicaoForcedValue}
  772:         />
  773:       </div>
  774:     </div>
  775:   );
  776: }

   56: function safeParseJSON(s) {
   57:   try {
   58:     const obj = JSON.parse(s);
   59:     return obj && typeof obj === "object" ? obj : null;
   60:   } catch {
   61:     return null;
   62:   }
   63: }
   64: 
   65: function loadSessionObj() {
   66:   try {
   67:     const raw = localStorage.getItem(ACCOUNT_SESSION_KEY);
   68:     if (!raw) return null;
   69:     const s = String(raw || "").trim();
   70:     if (!s.startsWith("{")) return null;
   71:     return safeParseJSON(s);
   72:   } catch {
   73:     return null;
   74:   }
   75: }

   77: function normalizeSessionPlan(plan) {
   78:   const p = String(plan || "").trim().toUpperCase();
   79:   if (p === "VIP") return "VIP";
   80:   if (p === "PREMIUM") return "PREMIUM";
   81:   if (p === "FREE") return "FREE";
   82:   return "";
   83: }
   84: 
   85: function getSessionKind(sess) {
   86:   const s = sess || loadSessionObj();
   87:   if (!s || s.ok !== true) return "anon";
   88: 
   89:   const type = String(s.type || "").trim().toLowerCase();
   90:   if (type === "guest") return "guest";
   91:   if (type === "user") return "user";
   92: 
   93:   const loginType = String(s.loginType || "").trim().toLowerCase();
   94:   const loginId = String(s.loginId || "").trim().toLowerCase();
   95:   const mode = String(s.mode || "").trim().toLowerCase();
   96: 
   97:   if (
   98:     loginType === "guest" ||
   99:     loginId === "guest" ||
  100:     s.skipped === true ||
  101:     mode === "skip"
  102:   ) {
  103:     return "guest";
  104:   }
  105: 
  106:   if (loginType === "user" || loginId === "user") {
  107:     return "user";
  108:   }
  109: 
  110:   if (s.uid || s.email) return "user";
  111: 
  112:   return "anon";
  113: }
  114: 
  115: function getSessionPlan(sess) {
  116:   const s = sess || loadSessionObj();
  117:   if (!s || s.ok !== true) return "";
  118: 
  119:   const normalized = normalizeSessionPlan(
  120:     s.plan ??
  121:       s.profile?.plan ??
  122:       s.subscription?.plan ??
  123:       s.account?.plan ??
  124:       s.customClaims?.plan ??
  125:       s.claims?.plan ??
  126:       s.appData?.plan ??
  127:       s.metadata?.plan
  128:   );
  129: 
  130:   if (normalized) return normalized;
  131: 
  132:   const kind = getSessionKind(s);
  133:   if (kind === "guest") return "FREE";
  134:   if (kind === "user") return "PREMIUM";
  135: 
  136:   return "";
  137: }

  167: function preloadImage(src) {
  168:   return new Promise((resolve, reject) => {
  169:     if (!src) return reject(new Error("src vazio"));
  170:     const img = new Image();
  171:     img.onload = () => resolve(true);
  172:     img.onerror = () => reject(new Error("falha ao carregar"));
  173:     img.src = src;
  174:   });
  175: }

  181: function normalizeHourBucket(h) {
  182:   const raw = String(h ?? "").trim();
  183:   if (!raw || raw.toLowerCase() === "todos") return null;
  184: 
  185:   const s = raw.replace(/\s+/g, "").toUpperCase();
  186: 
  187:   let m = s.match(/^(\d{1,2})(?:H|HS)$/);
  188:   if (m) return `${String(m[1]).padStart(2, "0")}h`;
  189: 
  190:   m = s.match(/^(\d{1,2}):(\d{2})(?::\d{2})?$/);
  191:   if (m) return `${String(m[1]).padStart(2, "0")}h`;
  192: 
  193:   m = s.match(/^(\d{1,2})$/);
  194:   if (m) return `${String(m[1]).padStart(2, "0")}h`;
  195: 
  196:   m = s.match(/(\d{1,2})/);
  197:   if (m) {
  198:     const hh = Number(m[1]);
  199:     if (Number.isFinite(hh) && hh >= 0 && hh <= 23) return `${String(hh).padStart(2, "0")}h`;
  200:   }
  201: 
  202:   return null;
  203: }
  204: 
  205: function normalizePositions(pos) {
  206:   const s = String(pos || "").trim();
  207:   if (!s || s.toLowerCase() === "todos") return [1, 2, 3, 4, 5, 6, 7];
  208: 
  209:   const m = s.match(/^(\d+)/);
  210:   if (m) {
  211:     const n = Number(m[1]);
  212:     if (Number.isFinite(n) && n >= 1 && n <= 7) return [n];
  213:   }
  214: 
  215:   return [1, 2, 3, 4, 5, 6, 7];
  216: }
  217: 
  218: function pad2(n) {
  219:   return String(n).padStart(2, "0");
  220: }

  222: function isISODate(s) {
  223:   return /^(\d{4})-(\d{2})-(\d{2})$/.test(String(s || ""));
  224: }

  226: function normalizeISO10(v) {
  227:   const s = String(v || "").trim();
  228:   const m = s.match(/^(\d{4}-\d{2}-\d{2})/);
  229:   const iso = m ? m[1] : "";
  230:   return isISODate(iso) ? iso : null;
  231: }

  276: function ymdToWeekdayShortPT(ymd) {
  277:   const m = String(ymd || "").match(/^(\d{4})-(\d{2})-(\d{2})$/);
  278:   if (!m) return null;
  279: 
  280:   const y = Number(m[1]);
  281:   const mo = Number(m[2]);
  282:   const d = Number(m[3]);
  283:   if (!y || !mo || !d) return null;
  284: 
  285:   const dt = new Date(Date.UTC(y, mo - 1, d));
  286:   const idx = dt.getUTCDay();
  287: 
  288:   const arr = ["Dom", "Seg", "Ter", "Qua", "Qui", "Sex", "Sáb"];
  289:   return arr[idx] || null;
  290: }

  301: function getLabelByGrupo(g) {
  302:   const n = Number(g);
  303:   if (!Number.isFinite(n) || n < 1 || n > 25) return "";
  304:   try {
  305:     if (typeof getAnimalLabelFromMap === "function") {
  306:       const a = getAnimalLabelFromMap({ grupo: n, animal: "" });
  307:       const sa = String(a ?? "").trim();
  308:       if (sa) return sa;
  309:     }
  310:   } catch {}
  311:   try {
  312:     if (typeof getAnimalLabelFromMap === "function") {
  313:       const b = getAnimalLabelFromMap(n);
  314:       const sb = String(b ?? "").trim();
  315:       if (sb) return sb;
  316:     }
  317:   } catch {}
  318:   return "";
  319: }

  321: function safeAnimalLabel({ grupo, animal }) {
  322:   try {
  323:     if (typeof getAnimalLabelFromMap === "function") {
  324:       const out = getAnimalLabelFromMap({ grupo, animal });
  325:       const s = String(out ?? "").trim();
  326:       if (s) return s;
  327:     }
  328:   } catch {}
  329:   const byGrupo = getLabelByGrupo(grupo);
  330:   if (byGrupo) return byGrupo;
  331:   return String(animal || "").trim();
  332: }

  334: function mapMetaRankingToRows(meta) {
  335:   const m = meta && typeof meta === "object" ? meta : null;
  336:   if (!m) return [];

  338:   const arr =
  339:     (Array.isArray(m.byGrupo) && m.byGrupo) ||
  340:     (Array.isArray(m.ranking) && m.ranking) ||
  341:     (Array.isArray(m.rows) && m.rows) ||
  342:     [];
  343:

  345:     .map((r) => ({
  346:       grupo: String(r?.grupo ?? r?.group ?? "").replace(/^0/, ""),
  347:       animal: r?.animal ?? r?.label ?? "",
  348:       total: Number(r?.apar ?? r?.total ?? r?.count ?? 0),

  353: function mapRankingJsonToApp(json) {
  354:   const safe = json && typeof json === "object" ? json : null;
  355:   if (!safe) return null;
  356: 
  357:   const totals = safe.totals || {};
  358:   const meta = safe.meta || {};
  359: 
  360:   const ranking = Array.isArray(safe.ranking) ? safe.ranking : [];
  361:   const byGrupo = Array.isArray(safe.byGrupo) ? safe.byGrupo : [];
  362: 
  363:   const rankingData = byGrupo.map((r) => ({
  364:     grupo: String(r?.grupo || "").replace(/^0/, ""),
  365:     animal: r?.animal || "",
  366:     total: Number(r?.apar || 0),
  367:     share: r?.share || "0.00%",
  368:   }));
  369: 
  370:   const top3 = ranking.slice(0, 3).map((r) => ({
  371:     grupo: String(r?.grupo || "").replace(/^0/, ""),
  372:     animal: r?.animal || "",
  373:     total: Number(r?.apar || 0),
  374:     share: r?.share || "0.00%",
  375:   }));
  376: 
  377:   const rankingMeta = {
  378:     mode: "json",
  379:     uf: meta.uf || null,
  380:     positionFilter: meta.positionFilter || "all",
  381:     dateFrom: meta.d1 || null,
  382:     dateTo: meta.d2 || null,
  383:     totalDraws: Number(totals.draws || 0),
  384:     totalOcorrencias: Number(totals.ocorrencias || 0),
  385:     totalDays:
  386:       Number(
  387:         totals.days ||
  388:           totals.dias ||
  389:           totals.uniqueDays ||
  390:           totals.unique_days ||
  391:           totals.unique_days ||
  392:           0
  393:       ) || 0,
  394:     generatedAt: meta.generatedAt || null,
  395:     top3,
  396:   };
  397: 
  398:   return { rankingData, rankingMeta, drawsRaw: [] };
  399: }

  463: function isIndexErrorMessage(err) {
  464:   const msg = String(err?.message || err || "").toLowerCase();
  465:   return (
  466:     msg.includes("failed-precondition") ||
  467:     msg.includes("failed_precondition") ||
  468:     msg.includes("requires an index") ||
  469:     (msg.includes("index") && msg.includes("create")) ||
  470:     (msg.includes("índice") && msg.includes("criar")) ||
  471:     (msg.includes("indice") && msg.includes("criar"))
  472:   );
  473: }

  475: function clampRangeToBounds(next, minDate, maxDate) {
  476:   const f = next?.from;
  477:   const t = next?.to;
  478: 
  479:   if (!isISODate(f) || !isISODate(t)) return null;
  480:   if (!minDate || !maxDate) return null;
  481:   if (!isISODate(minDate) || !isISODate(maxDate)) return null;
  482: 
  483:   let from = f < minDate ? minDate : f;
  484:   let to = t > maxDate ? maxDate : t;
  485: 
  486:   if (from > to) to = from;
  487:   return { from, to };
  488: }
  489: 
  490: function normalizeLoteriaKey(v) {
  491:   const raw = String(v ?? "").trim();
  492: 
  493:   if (!raw) return "PT_RIO";
  494: 
  495:   const key = raw
  496:     .toLowerCase()
  497:     .normalize("NFD")
  498:     .replace(/[\u0300-\u036f]/g, "")
  499:     .replace(/\s+/g, " ")
  500:     .trim();
  501: 
  502:   if (key === "federal" || key === "fed" || key === "br" || key === "brasil") return "FEDERAL";
  503:   if (key === "rj" || key === "rio" || key === "pt_rio" || key === "pt-rio") return "PT_RIO";
  504: 
  505:   const out = key
  506:     .toUpperCase()
  507:     .replace(/[^A-Z0-9]+/g, "_")
  508:     .replace(/^_+|_+$/g, "");
  509: 
  510:   return out || "PT_RIO";
  511: }

  513: function getDefaultFloorByUf(ufKey) {
  514:   const u = String(ufKey || "").trim().toUpperCase();
  515: 
  516:   if (u === "PT_RIO" || u === "RJ" || u === "RIO" || u === "PT-RIO") {
  517:     return "2022-06-07";
  518:   }
  519: 
  520:   if (u === "FEDERAL" || u === "FED" || u === "BR" || u === "BRASIL") {
  521:     return "2022-06-08";
  522:   }
  523: 
  524:   if (u === "LOOK") {
  525:     return "2022-06-07";
  526:   }
  527: 
  528:   if (u === "NACIONAL") {
  529:     return "2022-06-07";
  530:   }
  531: 
  532:   return "2022-01-01";
  533: }

  535: function getTodayYmdSafe() {
  536:   const ymd = normalizeToYMD_SP(new Date());
  537:   if (ymd && isISODate(ymd)) return ymd;
  538: 
  539:   const now = new Date();
  540:   const yyyy = now.getFullYear();
  541:   const mm = String(now.getMonth() + 1).padStart(2, "0");
  542:   const dd = String(now.getDate()).padStart(2, "0");
  543:   return `${yyyy}-${mm}-${dd}`;
  544: }
  545: 
  546: function yearRangeToDates(minY, maxY, floorISO, ceilISO) {
  547:   const y1 = Number(minY);
  548:   const y2 = Number(maxY);
  549:   if (!Number.isFinite(y1) || !Number.isFinite(y2)) return null;
  550: 
  551:   const a = Math.min(y1, y2);
  552:   const b = Math.max(y1, y2);
  553: 
  554:   let from = `${a}-01-01`;
  555:   let to = `${b}-12-31`;
  556: 
  557:   if (isISODate(floorISO) && from < floorISO) from = floorISO;
  558:   if (isISODate(ceilISO) && to > ceilISO) to = ceilISO;
  559: 
  560:   if (isISODate(from) && isISODate(to) && from > to) to = from;
  561:   return { from, to };
  562: }

  564: function normalizeCloseHourForKpi(raw) {
  565:   const s = String(raw ?? "").trim();
  566:   if (!s) return "";
  567: 
  568:   const bucket = normalizeHourBucket(s);
  569:   if (bucket) {
  570:     const hh = String(bucket).replace(/\D/g, "").padStart(2, "0");
  571:     return `${hh}:00`;
  572:   }
  573: 
  574:   let m = s.match(/^(\d{1,2}):(\d{2})/);
  575:   if (m) return `${String(m[1]).padStart(2, "0")}:00`;
  576: 
  577:   m = s.match(/^(\d{1,2})/);
  578:   if (m) return `${String(m[1]).padStart(2, "0")}:00`;
  579: 
  580:   return "";
  581: }

  583: function getDrawUniqKeyForKpi(d, ufKey, idx) {
  584:   if (!d) return null;
  585: 
  586:   const ymd = getDrawDate(d);
  587: 
  588:   const chRaw =
  589:     d.close_hour ??
  590:     d.closeHour ??
  591:     d.close_hour_raw ??
  592:     d.closeHourRaw ??
  593:     d.close_hour_bucket ??
  594:     d.closeHourBucket ??
  595:     d.slot ??
  596:     d.hour ??
  597:     d.hora ??
  598:     "";
  599: 
  600:   const ch = normalizeCloseHourForKpi(chRaw);
  601: 
  602:   const uf = String(ufKey ?? d.uf ?? d.lottery_key ?? d.lotteryKey ?? "")
  603:     .trim()
  604:     .toUpperCase() || "UF";
  605: 
  606:   if (ymd && ch) return `UF__${uf}__${ymd}__${ch}`;
  607: 
  608:   const drawId = String(d.drawId ?? d.draw_id ?? d.lottery_id ?? d.id ?? "").trim();
  609:   if (drawId) return `UF__${uf}__DRAWID__${drawId}`;
  610: 
  611:   if (ymd) return `UF__${uf}__${ymd}__IDX__${Number.isFinite(idx) ? idx : 0}`;
  612: 
  613:   return null;
  614: }

  616: function dedupeDrawsForKpi(list, ufKey) {
  617:   const arr = Array.isArray(list) ? list : [];
  618:   if (!arr.length) return [];
  619:   const seen = new Set();
  620:   const out = [];
  621: 
  622:   for (let i = 0; i < arr.length; i += 1) {
  623:     const d = arr[i];
  624:     const k = getDrawUniqKeyForKpi(d, ufKey, i);
  625:     if (!k) continue;
  626:     if (seen.has(k)) continue;
  627:     seen.add(k);
  628:     out.push(d);
  629:   }
  630: 
  631:   return out;
  632: }
  633: 
  634: function buildYearsAvailable(minISO, maxISO) {
  635:   if (!isISODate(minISO) || !isISODate(maxISO)) return [];
  636:   const y1 = Number(String(minISO).slice(0, 4));
  637:   const y2 = Number(String(maxISO).slice(0, 4));
  638:   if (!Number.isFinite(y1) || !Number.isFinite(y2)) return [];
  639:   const a = Math.min(y1, y2);
  640:   const b = Math.max(y1, y2);
  641:   const out = [];
  642:   for (let y = a; y <= b; y++) out.push(y);
  643:   return out;
  644: }
  645: 
  646: /* =========================
  647:    ✅ Persistência por UF
  648: ========================= */
  649: function makeDashStateKeyV2(ufKey) {
  650:   const u = String(ufKey || "").trim().toUpperCase() || "PT_RIO";
  651:   return `${DASH_STATE_KEY_BASE}__${u}`;
  652: }
  653: 
  654: function loadDashStateV2(ufKey) {
  655:   const KEY = makeDashStateKeyV2(ufKey);
  656: 
  657:   const v2 = safeReadJSON(KEY);
  658:   if (v2 && typeof v2 === "object") return v2;
  659: 
  660:   const legacyV2 = safeReadJSON(DASH_STATE_KEY_BASE);
  661:   if (legacyV2 && typeof legacyV2 === "object") {
  662:     const migrated = { ...legacyV2 };
  663:     safeWriteJSON(KEY, migrated);
  664:     return migrated;
  665:   }
  666: 
  667:   const v1 = safeReadJSON(DASH_STATE_KEY_V1);
  668:   if (v1 && typeof v1 === "object") {
  669:     const migrated = {
  670:       v: 2,
  671:       selectedGrupo: v1.selectedGrupo ?? null,
  672:       selectedYears: Array.isArray(v1.selectedYears) ? v1.selectedYears : [],
  673:       dateRange: v1.dateRange ?? null,
  674:       dateRangeQuery: v1.dateRangeQuery ?? null,
  675:       followMax: v1.followMax ?? true,
  676:       lastBoundsMin: v1.lastBoundsMin ?? null,
  677:       lastBoundsMax: v1.lastBoundsMax ?? null,
  678:     };
  679:     safeWriteJSON(KEY, migrated);
  680:     return migrated;
  681:   }
  682: 
  683:   return null;
  684: }

  686: export default function Dashboard(props) {
  687:   const externalFilters = props && typeof props === "object" ? props.filters : null;
  688:   const externalSetFilters = props && typeof props === "object" ? props.setFilters : null;
  689: 
  690:   const fallbackFilters = useMemo(
  691:     () => ({
  692:       loteria: "PT_RIO",
  693:       mes: "Todos",
  694:       diaMes: "Todos",
  695:       diaSemana: "Todos",
  696:       horario: "Todos",
  697:       animal: "Todos",
  698:       posicao: "Todos",
  699:     }),
  700:     []
  701:   );
  702: 
  703:   const [, setSessionObj] = useState(() => loadSessionObj());
  704:   const [sessionKind, setSessionKind] = useState(() => getSessionKind(loadSessionObj()));
  705:   const [, setSessionPlan] = useState(() => getSessionPlan(loadSessionObj()));
  706: 
  707:   const isGuest = sessionKind === "guest";
  708: 
  709:   useEffect(() => {
  710:     const serializeSession = (sess) => {
  711:       try {
  712:         return JSON.stringify(sess || null);
  713:       } catch {
  714:         return "__SERIALIZE_ERROR__";
  715:       }
  716:     };
  717: 
  718:     let lastSig = serializeSession(loadSessionObj());
  719: 
  720:     const refresh = () => {
  721:       const sess = loadSessionObj();
  722:       const nextSig = serializeSession(sess);
  723: 
  724:       if (nextSig === lastSig) return;
  725: 
  726:       lastSig = nextSig;
  727:       setSessionObj(sess);
  728:       setSessionKind(getSessionKind(sess));
  729:       setSessionPlan(getSessionPlan(sess));
  730:     };
  731: 
  732:     const onStorage = (e) => {
  733:       if (e && e.key && e.key !== ACCOUNT_SESSION_KEY) return;
  734:       refresh();
  735:     };
  736: 
  737:     const onVisibility = () => {
  738:       if (document.visibilityState === "visible") refresh();
  739:     };
  740: 
  741:     const onSessionChanged = () => refresh();
  742: 
  743:     window.addEventListener("storage", onStorage);
  744:     window.addEventListener("pp_session_changed", onSessionChanged);
  745:     document.addEventListener("visibilitychange", onVisibility);
  746: 
  747:     const t = setInterval(() => {
  748:       if (document.visibilityState !== "visible") return;
  749:       refresh();
  750:     }, SESSION_POLL_MS);
  751: 
  752:     return () => {
  753:       window.removeEventListener("storage", onStorage);
  754:       window.removeEventListener("pp_session_changed", onSessionChanged);
  755:       document.removeEventListener("visibilitychange", onVisibility);
  756:       clearInterval(t);
  757:     };
  758:   }, []);
  759: 
  760:   const rawFilters = useMemo(() => {
  761:     return externalFilters && typeof externalFilters === "object" ? externalFilters : fallbackFilters;
  762:   }, [externalFilters, fallbackFilters]);
  763: 
  764:   const filters = useMemo(() => {
  765:     return { ...fallbackFilters, ...rawFilters };
  766:   }, [rawFilters, fallbackFilters]);
  767: 
  768:   const noopSetFilters = useCallback(() => {}, []);
  769:   const setFilters = useMemo(() => {
  770:     return typeof externalSetFilters === "function" ? externalSetFilters : noopSetFilters;
  771:   }, [externalSetFilters, noopSetFilters]);
  772: 
  773:   const loteriaKey = useMemo(
  774:     () => normalizeLoteriaKey(filters?.lotteryKey || filters?.loteria || filters?.uf),
  775:     [filters?.lotteryKey, filters?.loteria, filters?.uf]
  776:   );
  777:

  779:

  781:   const locationLabel = isFederal
  782:     ? `FEDERAL (Brasil)${fedBucket ? ` — ${fedBucket}` : ""}`
  783:     : `${loteriaKey} — Brasil`;
  784:

  786: 
  787:   const [savedDashState, setSavedDashState] = useState(() => loadDashStateV2(uf));
  788: 
  789:   useEffect(() => {

  792:

  893:

  895:

  897:         const rq = restoredQueryRef.current;

  931:           setDateRangeQuery(fixed);

  936:           }
  937: 
  938:           return;
  939:         }
  940:

  942:         setDateRange(init);
  943:         setDateRangeQuery(init);
  944:         setSelectedYears([]);
  945:         setFollowMax(true);

  955:         const rawMax = String(res?.maxYmd ?? res?.maxDate ?? "").trim();

  991:             e?.message || String(e)
  992:           }`,
  993:         });
  994: 
  995:         initRangeIfNeeded(minYmd, maxYmd);
  996:       }

 1000:       alive = false;

 1195:   const closeHourBucketLocal = useMemo(() => normalizeHourBucket(filters.horario), [filters.horario]);

 1197: 
 1198:   const findGrupoByAnimalLabel = useCallback((animalLabel) => {

 1763:           <div className="dashTopRight" style={{ position: "relative", zIndex: 5, pointerEvents: "auto" }}>
 1764:             {uiBlockedByBounds ? (
 1765:               <PremiumTopRightSkeleton message={boundsMessage} />
 1766:             ) : (
 1767:               <>
 1768: 
 1769:                 {MIN_DATE && MAX_DATE && dateRange ? (
 1770:                   <div style={{ position: "relative", zIndex: 10, pointerEvents: "auto" }}>
 1771:                     <div style={{ position: "relative" }}>
 1772:                       <DateRangeControl
 1773:                         value={dateRange}
 1774:                         onChange={applyDateRange}
 1775:                         minDate={MIN_DATE}
 1776:                         maxDate={MAX_DATE}
 1777:                         years={yearsAvailable}
 1778:                         selectedYears={selectedYears}
 1779:                         onToggleYear={onToggleYear}
 1780:                         onClearYears={onClearYears}
 1781:                       />
 1782:                     </div>
 1783:                   </div>
 1784:                 ) : (
 1785:                   <PremiumInfoBox
 1786:                     title="Período indisponível"
 1787:                     description={boundsMessage || "Não foi possível resolver o intervalo de datas."}
 1788:                     extra="Se isso persistir, valide se todos os docs possuem o campo 'ymd' e se a coleção consultada é a mesma do app."
 1789:                   />
 1790:                 )}
 1791: 
 1792:                 {isHydrating ? (
 1793:                   hydratingBox
 1794:                 ) : isAggregatedOnly ? (
 1795:                   <>
 1796:                     {aggregatedInfoBox}
 1797:                     <KpiCards
 1798:                       items={kpiItems}
 1799:                       drawsRaw={dataReady ? drawsForView : []}
 1800:                       drawsRawGlobal={dataReady ? drawsForUi : []}
 1801:                       showGlobalAparicoes={true}
 1802:                       selectedGrupo={selectedGrupo}
 1803:                       selectedAnimalLabel={filters.animal}
 1804:                       selectedPosition={selectedPosition}
 1805:                     />
 1806:                   </>
 1807:                 ) : (
 1808:                   <KpiCards
 1809:                     items={kpiItems}
 1810:                     drawsRaw={dataReady ? drawsForView : []}
 1811:                     drawsRawGlobal={dataReady ? drawsForUi : []}
 1812:                     showGlobalAparicoes={true}
 1813:                     selectedGrupo={selectedGrupo}
 1814:                     selectedAnimalLabel={filters.animal}
 1815:                     selectedPosition={selectedPosition}
 1816:                   />
 1817:                 )}
 1818:               </>
 1819:             )}
 1820:           </div>
 1821:         </section>
 1822: 
 1823:         <section className="dashFilters">
 1824:           <div style={{ position: "relative" }}>
 1825:             <FiltersBar filters={filters} onChange={handleFilterChange} options={options} />
 1826:           </div>
 1827:         </section>
 1828: 
 1829:         <section className="dashCharts">
 1830:           {uiBlockedByBounds ? (
 1831:             <PremiumInfoBox
 1832:               title="Painel inicializando período"
 1833:               description="O sistema ainda está resolvendo o intervalo oficial da base. Assim que o range local estiver disponível, o painel libera o conteúdo."
 1834:               extra="Se houver período salvo ou consulta já resolvida, os blocos carregam sem esperar o bounds finalizar."
 1835:             />
 1836:           ) : rankingError && isIndexErrorMessage(rankingError) ? (
 1837:             <PremiumInfoBox
 1838:               title="Falha ao executar consulta (Firestore)"
 1839:               description="O Firestore retornou um erro típico de índice/consulta composta. Como os índices já estão criados, este erro costuma ser cache, query antiga ou divergência de campos."
 1840:               extra={indexErrorHint}
 1841:             />
 1842:           ) : isHydrating ? (
 1843:             hydratingBox
 1844:           ) : isAggregatedOnly ? (
 1845:             aggregatedNeedsDetailBox
 1846:           ) : emptyForRange ? (
 1847:             <PremiumInfoBox
 1848:               title="Sem registros no período / filtro atual"
 1849:               description="O período selecionado retornou zero draws. Ajuste o intervalo de datas ou filtros (horário, mês, dia, etc.) para voltar a exibir estatísticas."
 1850:               extra={`Dica rápida: ajuste o intervalo dentro de ${MIN_DATE || "—"} a ${MAX_DATE || "—"}.`}
 1851:             />
 1852:           ) : (
 1853:             <ChartsGrid
 1854:               drawsRaw={drawsForView}
 1855:               drawsRawGlobal={drawsForUi}
 1856:               rankingData={rankingFinal}
 1857:               rankingMeta={rankingMeta}
 1858:               filters={filters}
 1859:               loading={loadingEffective}
 1860:               error={rankingError}
 1861:               selectedGrupo={selectedGrupo}
 1862:               onSelectPosicao={onSelectPosicao}
 1863:             />
 1864:           )}
 1865:         </section>
 1866:       </main>
 1867:     </div>
 1868:   );
 1869: }

   94: export function getFederalPreferredHourForYmd(ymd) {
   95:   const y = String(ymd || "").trim();

  106: /**

  109: function isFederalInput(scopeInput) {
  110:   const up = String(scopeInput || "").trim().toUpperCase();
  111:   if (!up) return false;
  112:   if (up === FEDERAL_SCOPE_CODE) return true;
  113:   if (FEDERAL_INPUT_ALIASES.has(up)) return true;
  114: 
  115:   // normaliza espaços/underscores pra captar variações
  116:   const compact = up.replace(/[\s_]+/g, " ").trim();
  117:   return (
  118:     FEDERAL_INPUT_ALIASES.has(compact) ||
  119:     FEDERAL_INPUT_ALIASES.has(up.replace(/[\s_]+/g, "_"))
  120:   );
  121: }

  123: /**
  124:  * Resolve qual lottery_key usar quando o usuário passar:
  125:  * - "RJ" -> "PT_RIO"
  126:  * - "FEDERAL"/aliases -> "FEDERAL" (canônico)
  127:  * - outros -> mantém o valor original (compatível com seu app atual)
  128:  */
  129: function resolveLotteryKeyForQuery(ufInput) {
  130:   const uf = String(ufInput || "").trim();
  131:   if (!uf) return "";
  132:   const up = uf.toUpperCase();
  133: 
  134:   if (up === RJ_STATE_CODE) return RJ_LOTTERY_KEY;
  135:   if (isFederalInput(up)) return FEDERAL_SCOPE_CODE;
  136: 
  137:   return uf;
  138: }

  142:  * - "RJ" -> "PT_RIO"
  143:  * - Federal (qualquer alias) -> "FEDERAL"
  144:  * - outros -> UPPER(TRIM)
  145:  */
  146: function canonicalScopeKey(ufInput) {

  152:   if (isFederalInput(up)) return FEDERAL_SCOPE_CODE;

  154:   return up;
  155: }

  186:     if (isFederalInput(up)) return FEDERAL_SCOPE_CODE;
  187:     return up;

  191: }

  197: /**
  198:  * ✅ Normaliza "horário" para HH:MM
  199:  */
  200: function normalizeHourLike(value) {
  201:   const s0 = String(value ?? "").trim();
  202:   if (!s0) return "";
  203: 
  204:   const s = s0.replace(/\s+/g, "");
  205: 
  206:   const mhx = s.match(/^(\d{1,2})(?:h|hs|hr|hrs)$/i);
  207:   if (mhx) return `${pad2(mhx[1])}:00`;
  208: 
  209:   const mISO = s.match(/^(\d{1,2}):(\d{1,2})(?::(\d{1,2}))?$/);
  210:   if (mISO) return `${pad2(mISO[1])}:${pad2(mISO[2])}`;
  211: 
  212:   const mHm = s.match(/^(\d{3,4})$/);
  213:   if (mHm) {
  214:     const hh = String(mHm[1]).slice(0, -2);
  215:     const mm = String(mHm[1]).slice(-2);
  216:     if (/^\d{1,2}$/.test(hh) && /^\d{2}$/.test(mm)) {
  217:       return `${pad2(hh)}:${mm}`;
  218:     }
  219:   }
  220: 
  221:   const m2 = s.match(/^(\d{1,2})$/);
  222:   if (m2) return `${pad2(m2[1])}:00`;
  223: 
  224:   return s0.trim();
  225: }
  226: 
  227: function mhxToInt(v) {
  228:   const n = Number(v);
  229:   return Number.isFinite(n) ? n : 0;
  230: }
  231: 
  232: /**
  233:  * ✅ Bucket "09h", "11h"…
  234:  */
  235: function toHourBucket(value) {
  236:   const s = String(value ?? "").trim();
  237:   if (!s || s.toLowerCase() === "todos") return null;
  238: 
  239:   const mh = s.match(/^(\d{1,2})h$/i);
  240:   if (mh) return `${pad2(mhxToInt(mh[1]))}h`;
  241: 
  242:   const m1 = s.match(/^(\d{1,2}):(\d{2})$/);
  243:   if (m1) return `${pad2(mhxToInt(m1[1]))}h`;
  244: 
  245:   const m2 = s.match(/^(\d{1,2})$/);
  246:   if (m2) return `${pad2(mhxToInt(m2[1]))}h`;
  247: 
  248:   const m3 = s.match(/^(\d{1,2}):(\d{2}):(\d{2})$/);
  249:   if (m3) return `${pad2(mhxToInt(m3[1]))}h`;
  250: 
  251:   const m4 = s.match(/^(\d{3,4})$/);
  252:   if (m4) return `${pad2(mhxToInt(String(m4[1]).slice(0, -2)))}h`;
  253: 
  254:   return null;
  255: }
  256: 
  257: /* =========================
  258:    ✅ FIX DEFINITIVO: filtro de horário (bucket vs exato)
  259:    - bucket SOMENTE via closeHourBucket
  260:    - exact  SOMENTE via closeHour
  261:    (elimina ambiguidade e "filtro fantasma")
  262: ========================= */
  263: 
  264: function resolveHourFilter({ closeHour = null, closeHourBucket = null }) {
  265:   const bucket = toHourBucket(closeHourBucket);
  266:   if (bucket) return { kind: "bucket", bucket, hhmm: null };
  267: 
  268:   const hhmm = closeHour ? normalizeHourLike(closeHour) : null;
  269:   if (hhmm) return { kind: "exact", bucket: null, hhmm };
  270: 
  271:   return { kind: null, bucket: null, hhmm: null };
  272: }
  273: 
  274: function drawPassesHourFilter(draw, hourFilter) {
  275:   if (!hourFilter || !hourFilter.kind) return true;
  276: 
  277:   const raw =
  278:     draw?.close_hour ?? draw?.closeHour ?? draw?.hour ?? draw?.hora ?? "";
  279:   const norm = normalizeHourLike(raw);
  280: 
  281:   if (hourFilter.kind === "exact") {
  282:     return norm === normalizeHourLike(hourFilter.hhmm);
  283:   }
  284: 
  285:   if (hourFilter.kind === "bucket") {
  286:     return toHourBucket(norm) === toHourBucket(hourFilter.bucket);
  287:   }
  288: 
  289:   return true;
  290: }

  308: function normalizeLotteryCodeAny(d) {
  309:   const raw =
  310:     d?.lottery_code ??
  311:     d?.lotteryCode ??
  312:     d?.lot_code ??
  313:     d?.lotCode ??
  314:     d?.lot ??
  315:     d?.code ??
  316:     null;
  317:   const up = raw ? String(raw).trim().toUpperCase() : "";
  318:   return up || "";
  319: }
  320: 
  321: function sortDrawsLocal(draws) {
  322:   const toNum = (h) => {
  323:     const s = normalizeHourLike(h);
  324:     const m = s.match(/^(\d{2}):(\d{2})$/);
  325:     if (!m) return 0;
  326:     return Number(m[1]) * 100 + Number(m[2]);
  327:   };
  328: 
  329:   return [...(draws || [])].sort((a, b) => {
  330:     const ya = String(a?.ymd || "");
  331:     const yb = String(b?.ymd || "");
  332:     if (ya !== yb) return ya.localeCompare(yb);
  333: 
  334:     const ha = toNum(a?.close_hour);
  335:     const hb = toNum(b?.close_hour);
  336:     if (ha !== hb) return ha - hb;
  337: 
  338:     const la = normalizeLotteryCodeAny(a);
  339:     const lb = normalizeLotteryCodeAny(b);
  340:     if (la !== lb) return la.localeCompare(lb);
  341: 
  342:     const ia = String(a?.drawId || a?.id || "");
  343:     const ib = String(b?.drawId || b?.id || "");
  344:     return ia.localeCompare(ib);
  345:   });
  346: }
  347: 
  348: function sortDrawsLocalDesc(draws) {
  349:   const toNum = (h) => {
  350:     const s = normalizeHourLike(h);
  351:     const m = s.match(/^(\d{2}):(\d{2})$/);
  352:     if (!m) return 0;
  353:     return Number(m[1]) * 100 + Number(m[2]);
  354:   };
  355: 
  356:   return [...(draws || [])].sort((a, b) => {
  357:     const ya = String(a?.ymd || "");
  358:     const yb = String(b?.ymd || "");
  359:     if (ya !== yb) return yb.localeCompare(ya);
  360: 
  361:     const ha = toNum(a?.close_hour);
  362:     const hb = toNum(b?.close_hour);
  363:     if (ha !== hb) return hb - ha;
  364: 
  365:     const la = normalizeLotteryCodeAny(a);
  366:     const lb = normalizeLotteryCodeAny(b);
  367:     if (la !== lb) return lb.localeCompare(la);
  368: 
  369:     const ia = String(a?.drawId || a?.id || "");
  370:     const ib = String(b?.drawId || b?.id || "");
  371:     return ib.localeCompare(ia);
  372:   });
  373: }
  374: 
  375: /**
  376:  * ✅ DEDUPE
  377:  * - chave lógica: ymd + hour (+ lottery_code quando existir)
  378:  * - se não houver ymd/hour, cai no id/idx
  379:  *
  380:  * ✅ FIX CRÍTICO:
  381:  * Não colapsar dois draws distintos do mesmo dia/horário com lottery_code diferente.
  382:  */
  383: function dedupeDrawsLocal(draws) {
  384:   const arr = Array.isArray(draws) ? draws : [];
  385: 
  386:   const byKey = new Map();
  387:   const order = [];
  388: 
  389:   function score(d) {
  390:     const prizesLen = Array.isArray(d?.prizes) ? d.prizes.length : 0;
  391:     const pc = Number.isFinite(Number(d?.prizesCount))
  392:       ? Number(d.prizesCount)
  393:       : 0;
  394:     const hasLogical = !!(d?.ymd && d?.close_hour);
  395:     return prizesLen * 1_000_000 + pc * 1_000 + (hasLogical ? 10 : 0);
  396:   }
  397: 
  398:   for (let i = 0; i < arr.length; i += 1) {
  399:     const raw = arr[i] || {};
  400: 
  401:     const ymd = raw.ymd || normalizeToYMD(raw.date) || "";
  402:     const hour = normalizeHourLike(raw.close_hour || raw.closeHour || "");
  403: 
  404:     const lotCode = normalizeLotteryCodeAny(raw);
  405: 
  406:     const drawId = raw.drawId ?? raw.id ?? raw.__name__ ?? null;
  407:     const idStr = drawId != null ? String(drawId) : "";
  408: 
  409:     const hasLogical = !!(ymd && hour);
  410: 
  411:     const key = hasLogical
  412:       ? lotCode
  413:         ? `${ymd}__${hour}__${lotCode}`
  414:         : `${ymd}__${hour}`
  415:       : `id__${idStr || `idx_${i}`}`;
  416: 
  417:     const normalized = {
  418:       ...raw,
  419:       ymd: ymd || raw.ymd || null,
  420:       close_hour: hour || raw.close_hour || raw.closeHour || "",
  421:       closeHour: hour || raw.closeHour || raw.close_hour || "",
  422:       lottery_code: lotCode || raw.lottery_code || raw.lotteryCode || null,
  423:       drawId: raw.drawId || raw.id || raw.__name__ || null,
  424:       id: raw.id || raw.drawId || raw.__name__ || null,
  425:     };
  426: 
  427:     if (!byKey.has(key)) {
  428:       byKey.set(key, normalized);
  429:       order.push(key);
  430:       continue;
  431:     }
  432: 
  433:     const prev = byKey.get(key);
  434:     if (score(normalized) > score(prev)) {
  435:       byKey.set(key, normalized);
  436:     }
  437:   }
  438: 
  439:   return order.map((k) => byKey.get(k)).filter(Boolean);
  440: }
  441: 
  442: async function mapWithConcurrency(items, limitN, mapper) {
  443:   const arr = Array.isArray(items) ? items : [];
  444:   const concurrency = Math.max(1, Number(limitN) || 6);
  445:   const results = new Array(arr.length);
  446:   let idx = 0;
  447: 
  448:   async function worker() {
  449:     while (true) {
  450:       const current = idx;
  451:       idx += 1;
  452:       if (current >= arr.length) break;
  453:       results[current] = await mapper(arr[current], current);
  454:     }
  455:   }
  456: 
  457:   await Promise.all(
  458:     Array.from({ length: Math.min(concurrency, arr.length) }, () => worker())
  459:   );
  460:   return results;
  461: }
  462: 
  463: /* =========================
  464:    ✅ Leitura: smart (custo baixo)
  465: ========================= */
  466: 
  467: function isIndexError(error) {
  468:   const msg = String(error?.message || "").toLowerCase();
  469:   const code = String(error?.code || "").toLowerCase();
  470:   return (
  471:     code.includes("failed-precondition") ||
  472:     msg.includes("failed_precondition") ||
  473:     (msg.includes("index") && msg.includes("create")) ||
  474:     (msg.includes("requires") && msg.includes("index"))
  475:   );
  476: }
  477: 
  478: function getDocDateRaw(d) {
  479:   if (!d) return null;
  480:   return d.ymd ?? d.date ?? d.data ?? d.dt ?? d.draw_date ?? d.close_date ?? null;
  481: }
  482: 
  483: /* =========================
  484:    Prizes (ROBUSTO + CACHE)
  485: ========================= */
  486: 
  487: function prizesCacheKeyAll(drawId) {
  488:   return `all::${String(drawId)}`;
  489: }
  490: 
  491: /**
  492:  * ✅ FIX DEFINITIVO:
  493:  * Se existir embeddedPrizes mas vier "agregado/incompleto" (sem grupo/posição válidos),
  494:  * faz fallback e busca a subcollection draws/{id}/prizes.
  495:  */
  496: async function fetchPrizesForDraw(drawId, positionsArr, embeddedPrizes) {
  497:   const drawKey = String(drawId || "").trim();
  498:   if (!drawKey) return [];
  499: 
  500:   if (Array.isArray(embeddedPrizes) && embeddedPrizes.length) {
  501:     const normalized = embeddedPrizes.map((p, idx) =>
  502:       normalizePrize(p, p?.prizeId ?? `emb_${idx}`)
  503:     );
  504: 
  505:     const cleaned = normalized.filter(
  506:       (x) => isValidGrupo(x?.grupo) && isValidPosition(x?.position)
  507:     );
  508: 
  509:     if (cleaned.length) {
  510:       const allSorted = sortPrizesByPosition(cleaned);
  511:       return filterPrizesByPositions(allSorted, positionsArr);
  512:     }
  513:   }
  514: 
  515:   const allKey = prizesCacheKeyAll(drawKey);
  516:   const cachedAll = cacheGet(PRIZES_CACHE, allKey);
  517:   if (Array.isArray(cachedAll) && cachedAll.length > 0) {
  518:     return filterPrizesByPositions(cachedAll, positionsArr);
  519:   }
  520: 
  521:   const prizesCol = collection(db, "draws", drawKey, "prizes");
  522: 
  523:   const { snap, error } = await safeGetDocsSmart(prizesCol, { policy: "server" });
  524:   if (error) throw error;
  525: 
  526:   const allRaw = snap.docs.map((d) => normalizePrize(d.data(), d.id));
  527: 
  528:   // console.log("DEBUG allRaw:", allRaw);
  529: 
  530: const all = allRaw.filter(
  531:   (x) => isValidGrupo(x?.grupo) && isValidPosition(x?.position)
  532: );
  533: 
  534: // console.log("DEBUG allFiltered:", all);
  535: 
  536:   const allSorted = sortPrizesByPosition(all);
  537:   cacheSet(PRIZES_CACHE, allKey, allSorted);
  538: 
  539:   return filterPrizesByPositions(allSorted, positionsArr);
  540: }
  541: 
  542: /* =========================
  543:    Map Draw
  544: ========================= */
  545: 
  546: function mapDrawDoc(doc) {
  547:   const d = doc.data();
  548: 
  549:   const ymd = d.ymd || normalizeToYMD(getDocDateRaw(d));
  550:   const hourNorm = normalizeHourLike(
  551:     d.close_hour ?? d.closeHour ?? d.hour ?? d.hora ?? ""
  552:   );
  553: 
  554:   const embeddedPrizes = Array.isArray(d.prizes) && d.prizes.length > 0 ? d.prizes : null;
  555: 
  556:   const ufRaw = d.uf ?? null;
  557:   const lotteryKeyRaw = d.lottery_key ?? d.lotteryKey ?? d.lottery ?? null;
  558: 
  559:   const lotteryCodeRaw =
  560:     d.lottery_code ??
  561:     d.lotteryCode ??
  562:     d.lot_code ??
  563:     d.lotCode ??
  564:     d.lot ??
  565:     d.code ??
  566:     d.lottery_id ??
  567:     d.lotteryId ??
  568:     null;
  569: 
  570:   return {
  571:     drawId: doc.id,
  572:     id: doc.id,
  573: 
  574:     date: d.date ?? d.data ?? d.dt ?? d.draw_date ?? d.close_date ?? null,
  575:     ymd,
  576: 
  577:     close_hour: hourNorm,
  578:     closeHour: hourNorm,
  579: 
  580:     uf: ufRaw,
  581:     lottery_key: lotteryKeyRaw,
  582: 
  583:     lottery_code: lotteryCodeRaw ? String(lotteryCodeRaw).toUpperCase() : null,
  584: 
  585:     prizesCount: d.prizesCount,
  586:     prizes: embeddedPrizes,
  587:   };
  588: }
