  590: /* =========================
  591:    Compat: uf OU lottery_key
  592: ========================= */
  593: 
  594: function buildUfWhereClauses(fieldName, uf) {
  595:   const normalized = normalizeValueForField(fieldName, uf);
  596:   return [where(fieldName, "==", normalized)];
  597: }
  598: 
  599: function normalizeOrderBys(extraOrderBy) {
  600:   if (!extraOrderBy) return [];
  601:   return Array.isArray(extraOrderBy)
  602:     ? extraOrderBy.filter(Boolean)
  603:     : [extraOrderBy];
  604: }
  605: 
  606: async function queryDrawsByField({
  607:   fieldName,
  608:   uf,
  609:   extraWheres = [],
  610:   extraOrderBy = null,
  611:   extraLimit = null,
  612:   policy = DEFAULT_READ_POLICY,
  613: }) {
  614:   const drawsCol = collection(db, "draws");
  615: 
  616:   const parts = [
  617:     drawsCol,
  618:     ...buildUfWhereClauses(fieldName, uf),
  619:     ...buildRJLockWhereIfNeeded(fieldName, uf),
  620:     ...(extraWheres || []),
  621:   ];
  622: 
  623:   const orderBys = normalizeOrderBys(extraOrderBy);
  624:   for (const ob of orderBys) parts.push(ob);
  625: 
  626:   if (extraLimit) parts.push(extraLimit);
  627: 
  628:   const qRef = query(...parts);
  629:   return safeGetDocsSmart(qRef, { policy });
  630: }
  631: 
  632: function extractUfParam(maybeUfOrObj) {
  633:   if (!maybeUfOrObj) return "";
  634:   if (typeof maybeUfOrObj === "string") return maybeUfOrObj;
  635:   if (typeof maybeUfOrObj === "object" && typeof maybeUfOrObj.uf === "string") {
  636:     return maybeUfOrObj.uf;
  637:   }
  638:   return String(maybeUfOrObj || "");
  639: }
  640: 
  641: async function fetchDrawDocsPreferUf({
  642:   uf,
  643:   extraWheres = [],
  644:   extraOrderBy = null,
  645:   extraLimit = null,
  646:   policy = DEFAULT_READ_POLICY,
  647: }) {
  648:   const ufTrim = String(extractUfParam(uf) || "").trim();
  649:   const ufUp = ufTrim.toUpperCase();
  650: 
  651:   if (isFederalInput(ufUp)) {
  652:     for (const lk of FEDERAL_LOTTERY_KEYS) {
  653:       const { snap, error } = await queryDrawsByField({
  654:         fieldName: "lottery_key",
  655:         uf: lk,
  656:         extraWheres,
  657:         extraOrderBy,
  658:         extraLimit,
  659:         policy,
  660:       });
  661: 
  662:       if (error) return { docs: [], usedField: "lottery_key", error };
  663:       if (snap?.docs?.length) {
  664:         return { docs: snap.docs, usedField: "lottery_key", error: null };
  665:       }
  666:     }
  667: 
  668:     return { docs: [], usedField: "lottery_key", error: null };
  669:   }
  670: 
  671:   {
  672:     const { snap, error } = await queryDrawsByField({
  673:       fieldName: "uf",
  674:       uf: ufUp,
  675:       extraWheres,
  676:       extraOrderBy,
  677:       extraLimit,
  678:       policy,
  679:     });
  680: 
  681:     if (!error && snap?.docs?.length) {
  682:       return { docs: snap.docs, usedField: "uf", error: null };
  683:     }
  684:     if (error) return { docs: [], usedField: "uf", error };
  685:   }
  686: 
  687:   {
  688:     const lotteryKey =
  689:       ufUp === RJ_STATE_CODE ? RJ_LOTTERY_KEY : resolveLotteryKeyForQuery(ufTrim);
  690: 
  691:     const { snap, error } = await queryDrawsByField({
  692:       fieldName: "lottery_key",
  693:       uf: String(lotteryKey).toUpperCase(),
  694:       extraWheres,
  695:       extraOrderBy,
  696:       extraLimit,
  697:       policy,
  698:     });
  699: 
  700:     if (!error && snap?.docs?.length) {
  701:       return { docs: snap.docs, usedField: "lottery_key", error: null };
  702:     }
  703:     if (error) return { docs: [], usedField: "lottery_key", error };
  704:   }
  705: 
  706:   return { docs: [], usedField: "none", error: null };
  707: }
  708: 
  709: /* =========================
  710:    Bounds (min/max reais)
  711: ========================= */
  712: 
  713: function pickMinMaxFromMapped(mapped) {
  714:   let minYmd = null;
  715:   let maxYmd = null;
  716: 
  717:   for (const d of mapped) {
  718:     const y = d?.ymd || normalizeToYMD(d?.date);
  719:     if (!y || !isYMD(y)) continue;
  720:     if (!minYmd || y < minYmd) minYmd = y;
  721:     if (!maxYmd || y > maxYmd) maxYmd = y;
  722:   }
  723: 
  724:   return { minYmd, maxYmd };
  725: }
  726: 
  727: /**
  728:  * ✅ “Prova de vida” do maxYmd sem depender de índice composto nem de docId:
  729:  * tenta dias recentes (==) desc até achar pelo menos 1 draw.
  730:  */
  731: async function probeRecentMaxYmd(uf, lookbackDays = 45) {
  732:   const base = utcTodayYmd();
  733:   const n = Math.max(3, Math.min(120, Number(lookbackDays) || 45));
  734: 
  735:   for (let i = 0; i <= n; i += 1) {
  736:     const day = addDaysUTCLocal(base, -i);
  737:     const { docs, error } = await fetchDrawDocsPreferUf({
  738:       uf,
  739:       extraWheres: [where("ymd", "==", day)],
  740:       policy: "server",
  741:       extraLimit: limit(5),
  742:     });
  743:     if (error) return { ok: false, maxYmd: null, error };
  744:     if (docs && docs.length) return { ok: true, maxYmd: day, error: null };
  745:   }
  746: 
  747:   return { ok: false, maxYmd: null, error: null };
  748: }
  749: 
  750: /**
  751:  * ✅ API bounds (backend) — fonte única preferencial
  752:  */
  753: async function fetchBoundsFromApi(ufOrObj) {
  754:   const uf = String(extractUfParam(ufOrObj) || "").trim();
  755:   if (!uf) return { ok: false, error: new Error("uf vazio") };
  756: 
  757:   const lot = canonicalScopeKey(uf) || String(uf).trim().toUpperCase();
  758:   const url = apiUrl(`/api/bounds?lottery=${encodeURIComponent(lot)}`);
  759:   try {
  760:     const r = await fetch(url, { method: "GET" });
  761:     const j = await r.json().catch(() => null);
  762: 
  763:     if (!r.ok || !j || !j.ok) {
  764:       return {
  765:         ok: false,
  766:         error: new Error(`bounds api not ok: ${j?.message || r.status}`),
  767:         payload: j || null,
  768:       };
  769:     }
  770: 
  771:     const minYmd = String(j.minYmd || "").trim();
  772:     const maxYmd = String(j.maxYmd || "").trim();
  773:     if (!isYMD(minYmd) || !isYMD(maxYmd)) {
  774:       return {
  775:         ok: false,
  776:         error: new Error("bounds api retornou min/max inválidos"),
  777:         payload: j,
  778:       };
  779:     }
  780: 
  781:     return {
  782:       ok: true,
  783:       uf,
  784:       lottery: String(j.lottery || lot).trim(),
  785:       minYmd,
  786:       maxYmd,
  787:       minDocId: j.minDocId || null,
  788:       maxDocId: j.maxDocId || null,
  789:       source: String(j.source || "api_bounds"),
  790:     };
  791:   } catch (e) {
  792:     return { ok: false, error: e };
  793:   }
  794: }
  795: 
  796: export async function getKingBoundsByUf(ufOrObj) {
  797:   const uf = String(extractUfParam(ufOrObj) || "").trim();
  798:   if (!uf) throw new Error("Parâmetro obrigatório: uf");
  799: 
  800:   const apiTry = await fetchBoundsFromApi(ufOrObj);
  801:   if (apiTry?.ok) {
  802:     const scopeKey = canonicalScopeKey(uf);
  803:     const floored = applyBoundsFloor(scopeKey, {
  804:       minYmd: apiTry.minYmd,
  805:       maxYmd: apiTry.maxYmd,
  806:     });
  807: 
  808:     return {
  809:       ok: true,
  810:       uf,
  811: 
  812:       minYmd: floored.minYmd,
  813:       maxYmd: floored.maxYmd,
  814: 
  815:       minDate: floored.minYmd,
  816:       maxDate: floored.maxYmd,
  817: 
  818:       minDocId: apiTry.minDocId || null,
  819:       maxDocId: apiTry.maxDocId || null,
  820: 
  821:       source: `api_bounds:${apiTry.source}`,
  822:     };
  823:   }
  824: 
  825:   const SCAN_LIMIT = 50;
  826:   const FALLBACK_EDGE_LIMIT = 600;
  827: 
  828:   let firstTryError = null;
  829: 
  830:   const DOC_ID = documentId();
  831: 
  832:   async function sampleEdgesByDocId(fieldName, ufValue) {
  833:     const asc = await queryDrawsByField({
  834:       fieldName,
  835:       uf: ufValue,
  836:       extraOrderBy: [orderBy(DOC_ID)],
  837:       extraLimit: limit(FALLBACK_EDGE_LIMIT),
  838:       policy: "server",
  839:     });
  840: 
  841:     const last = await queryDrawsByField({
  842:       fieldName,
  843:       uf: ufValue,
  844:       extraOrderBy: [orderBy(DOC_ID)],
  845:       extraLimit: limitToLast(FALLBACK_EDGE_LIMIT),
  846:       policy: "server",
  847:     });
  848: 
  849:     const mappedA = (asc?.snap?.docs || []).map(mapDrawDoc);
  850:     const mappedB = (last?.snap?.docs || []).map(mapDrawDoc);
  851: 
  852:     const merged = dedupeDrawsLocal([...mappedA, ...mappedB]);
  853: 
  854:     return {
  855:       merged,
  856:       ok: merged.length > 0,
  857:       errors: [asc?.error, last?.error].filter(Boolean),
  858:       sources: [asc?.source, last?.source].filter(Boolean),
  859:     };
  860:   }
  861: 
  862:   {
  863:     const orderAsc = [orderBy("ymd", "asc"), orderBy(DOC_ID)];
  864:     const orderDesc = [orderBy("ymd", "desc"), orderBy(DOC_ID)];
  865: 
  866:     const {
  867:       docs: minDocs,
  868:       usedField: usedMin,
  869:       error: eMin,
  870:     } = await fetchDrawDocsPreferUf({
  871:       uf,
  872:       extraOrderBy: orderAsc,
  873:       extraLimit: limit(SCAN_LIMIT),
  874:       policy: "server",
  875:     });
  876: 
  877:     const {
  878:       docs: maxDocs,
  879:       usedField: usedMax,
  880:       error: eMax,
  881:     } = await fetchDrawDocsPreferUf({
  882:       uf,
  883:       extraOrderBy: orderDesc,
  884:       extraLimit: limit(SCAN_LIMIT),
  885:       policy: "server",
  886:     });
  887: 
  888:     if (eMin || eMax) {
  889:       if (!isIndexError(eMin) && !isIndexError(eMax)) firstTryError = eMin || eMax;
  890:     }
  891: 
  892:     if (minDocs.length && maxDocs.length && !eMin && !eMax) {
  893:       const mappedMin = minDocs.map(mapDrawDoc);
  894:       const mappedMax = maxDocs.map(mapDrawDoc);
  895: 
  896:       const { minYmd } = pickMinMaxFromMapped(mappedMin);
  897:       const { maxYmd } = pickMinMaxFromMapped(mappedMax);
  898: 
  899:       if (minYmd && maxYmd) {
  900:         return {
  901:           ok: true,
  902:           uf,
  903:           minYmd,
  904:           maxYmd,
  905:           minDate: minYmd,
  906:           maxDate: maxYmd,
  907:           source: `ymd_scan${SCAN_LIMIT}:${usedMin}/${usedMax}`,
  908:         };
  909:       }
  910:     }
  911:   }
  912: 
  913:   const recentProbe = await probeRecentMaxYmd(uf, 60);
  914:   const recentMaxYmd = recentProbe?.ok ? recentProbe.maxYmd : null;
  915: 
  916:   const scopeKeyBounds = canonicalScopeKey(uf);
  917:   {
  918:     const idxInfo = (() => {
  919:       const code = String(firstTryError?.code || "");
  920:       const msg = String(firstTryError?.message || "");
  921:       if (!code && !msg) return "";
  922:       return `:firstTryError=${code || "err"}`;
  923:     })();
  924: 
  925:     const a = await sampleEdgesByDocId("uf", String(uf).toUpperCase());
  926: 
  927:     const ufUp = String(uf || "").trim().toUpperCase();
  928: 
  929:     if (!a.ok && isFederalInput(ufUp)) {
  930:       for (const lk of FEDERAL_LOTTERY_KEYS) {
  931:         const b = await sampleEdgesByDocId("lottery_key", lk);
  932:         if (b.ok) {
  933:           const mm = pickMinMaxFromMapped(b.merged);
  934: 
  935:           const maxFixed =
  936:             recentMaxYmd && (!mm.maxYmd || recentMaxYmd > mm.maxYmd)
  937:               ? recentMaxYmd
  938:               : mm.maxYmd;
  939: 
  940:           const floored = applyBoundsFloor(scopeKeyBounds, {
  941:             minYmd: mm.minYmd || null,
  942:             maxYmd: maxFixed || null,
  943:           });
  944: 
  945:           return {
  946:             ok: !!(floored.minYmd && floored.maxYmd),
  947:             uf,
  948: 
  949:             minYmd: floored.minYmd,
  950:             maxYmd: floored.maxYmd,
  951: 
  952:             minDate: floored.minYmd,
  953:             maxDate: floored.maxYmd,
  954: 
  955:             source: `fallback_edges_docId_limit=${FALLBACK_EDGE_LIMIT}:lottery_key(${lk}):sampleCount=${b.merged.length}${
  956:               recentMaxYmd ? ":max_probe" : ""
  957:             }${idxInfo}`,
  958:           };
  959:         }
  960:       }
  961:     }
  962: 
  963:     const lotteryKey =
  964:       ufUp === RJ_STATE_CODE ? RJ_LOTTERY_KEY : resolveLotteryKeyForQuery(uf);
  965: 
  966:     const b = a.ok
  967:       ? null
  968:       : await sampleEdgesByDocId("lottery_key", String(lotteryKey).toUpperCase());
  969: 
  970:     const merged = a.ok ? a.merged : b?.merged || [];
  971:     const mm = pickMinMaxFromMapped(merged);
  972: 
  973:     const maxFixed =
  974:       recentMaxYmd && (!mm.maxYmd || recentMaxYmd > mm.maxYmd)
  975:         ? recentMaxYmd
  976:         : mm.maxYmd;
  977: 
  978:     const floored = applyBoundsFloor(scopeKeyBounds, {
  979:       minYmd: mm.minYmd || null,
  980:       maxYmd: maxFixed || null,
  981:     });
  982: 
  983:     return {
  984:       ok: !!(floored.minYmd && floored.maxYmd),
  985:       uf,
  986: 
  987:       minYmd: floored.minYmd,
  988:       maxYmd: floored.maxYmd,
  989: 
  990:       minDate: floored.minYmd,
  991:       maxDate: floored.maxYmd,
  992: 
  993:       source: `fallback_edges_docId_limit=${FALLBACK_EDGE_LIMIT}:${
  994:         a.ok ? "uf" : "lottery_key"
  995:       }:sampleCount=${merged.length}${recentMaxYmd ? ":max_probe" : ""}${idxInfo}`,
  996:     };
  997:   }
  998: }
  999: 
 1000: /* =========================
 1001:    API: Day
 1002: ========================= */
 1003: 
 1004: function buildPrizeSignature(draw) {
 1005:   const prizes = Array.isArray(draw?.prizes) ? draw.prizes : [];
 1006:   if (!prizes.length) return "";
 1007: 
 1008:   const parts = prizes
 1009:     .map((p) => {
 1010:       const pos = Number(p?.position);
 1011:       const grupo = Number(p?.grupo);
 1012:       const numero = toPrizeDigitsByPosition(
 1013:         p?.numero ?? p?.milhar ?? p?.number ?? p?.num ?? p?.valor ?? "",
 1014:         pos
 1015:       );
 1016: 
 1017:       if (!Number.isFinite(pos) || !Number.isFinite(grupo) || !numero) return "";
 1018:       return `${pad2(pos)}:${pad2(grupo)}:${numero}`;
 1019:     })
 1020:     .filter(Boolean)
 1021:     .sort();
 1022: 
 1023:   return parts.join("|");
 1024: }
 1025: 
 1026: function federalHourPriority(draw) {
 1027:   const h = normalizeHourLike(
 1028:     draw?.close_hour || draw?.closeHour || draw?.hour || draw?.hora || ""
 1029:   );
 1030:   const ymd = String(draw?.ymd || normalizeToYMD(draw?.date) || "").trim();
 1031:   const n = hourToNumSafe(h);
 1032: 
 1033:   const prefer20h = getFederalPreferredHourForYmd(ymd) === "20:00";
 1034: 
 1035:   if (prefer20h) {
 1036:     if (h === "20:00") return 200000 + n;
 1037:     if (h === "19:00") return 190000 + n;
 1038:     return 100000 + n;
 1039:   }
 1040: 
 1041:   if (h === "19:00") return 200000 + n;
 1042:   if (h === "20:00") return 190000 + n;
 1043:   return 100000 + n;
 1044: }
 1045: 
 1046: function pickBetterFederalClone(a, b) {
 1047:   const pa = Number.isFinite(Number(a?.prizesCount))
 1048:     ? Number(a.prizesCount)
 1049:     : Array.isArray(a?.prizes)
 1050:     ? a.prizes.length
 1051:     : 0;
 1052: 
 1053:   const pb = Number.isFinite(Number(b?.prizesCount))
 1054:     ? Number(b.prizesCount)
 1055:     : Array.isArray(b?.prizes)
 1056:     ? b.prizes.length
 1057:     : 0;
 1058: 
 1059:   if (pa !== pb) return pb > pa ? b : a;
 1060: 
 1061:   const ha = federalHourPriority(a);
 1062:   const hb = federalHourPriority(b);
 1063:   if (ha !== hb) return hb > ha ? b : a;
 1064: 
 1065:   const ia = String(a?.drawId || a?.id || "");
 1066:   const ib = String(b?.drawId || b?.id || "");
 1067:   return ib.localeCompare(ia) > 0 ? b : a;
 1068: }
 1069: 
 1070: function collapseFederalDailyCloneDraws(draws) {
 1071:   const arr = Array.isArray(draws) ? draws : [];
 1072:   const byKey = new Map();
 1073: 
 1074:   for (const d of arr) {
 1075:     const ymd = String(d?.ymd || normalizeToYMD(d?.date) || "").trim();
 1076:     const sig = buildPrizeSignature(d);
 1077: 
 1078:     if (!ymd || !sig) {
 1079:       const fallbackKey = `id::${String(
 1080:         d?.drawId || d?.id || Math.random()
 1081:       )}`;
 1082:       if (!byKey.has(fallbackKey)) byKey.set(fallbackKey, d);
 1083:       continue;
 1084:     }
 1085: 
 1086:     const key = `${ymd}__${sig}`;
 1087:     const prev = byKey.get(key);
 1088: 
 1089:     if (!prev) byKey.set(key, d);
 1090:     else byKey.set(key, pickBetterFederalClone(prev, d));
 1091:   }
 1092: 
 1093:   return sortDrawsLocal(Array.from(byKey.values()));
 1094: }
 1095: 
 1096: function drawsCacheKeyDay({ scopeKey, ymd, positionsArr, hourFilter }) {
 1097:   const p = positionsArr && positionsArr.length ? positionsArr.join(",") : "all";
 1098:   const h =
 1099:     hourFilter?.kind === "bucket"
 1100:       ? `bucket:${hourFilter.bucket}`
 1101:       : hourFilter?.kind === "exact"
 1102:       ? `exact:${hourFilter.hhmm}`
 1103:       : "all";
 1104:   return `day::${scopeKey}::${ymd}::pos=${p}::h=${h}`;
 1105: }
 1106: 
 1107: export async function getKingResultsByDate({
 1108:   uf,
 1109:   date,
 1110:   closeHour = null,
 1111:   closeHourBucket = null,
 1112:   positions = null,
 1113: }) {
 1114:   if (!uf || !date) throw new Error("Parâmetros obrigatórios: uf e date");
 1115: 
 1116:   const scopeKey = canonicalScopeKey(uf);
 1117: 
 1118:   const positionsArr = normalizePositions(positions);
 1119:   const ymdDate = normalizeToYMD(date);
 1120:   if (!ymdDate) return [];
 1121: 
 1122:   const hourFilter = resolveHourFilter({ closeHour, closeHourBucket });
 1123: 
 1124:   const dayKey = drawsCacheKeyDay({
 1125:     scopeKey,
 1126:     ymd: ymdDate,
 1127:     positionsArr,
 1128:     hourFilter,
 1129:   });
 1130: 
 1131:   const forceFreshServer = shouldForceFreshServerRead(ymdDate);
 1132:   const allowMemoryCache = !forceFreshServer;
 1133: 
 1134:   if (allowMemoryCache) {
 1135:     const cached = cacheGet(DRAWS_CACHE, dayKey);
 1136:     if (cached) return cached;
 1137:   }
 1138: 
 1139:   const readPolicy = forceFreshServer ? "server" : DEFAULT_READ_POLICY;
 1140: 
 1141:   const docCandidates = [];
 1142: 
 1143:   {
 1144:     const { docs: found, error } = await fetchDrawDocsPreferUf({
 1145:       uf,
 1146:       extraWheres: [where("ymd", "==", ymdDate)],
 1147:       policy: readPolicy,
 1148:     });
 1149:     if (error) throw error;
 1150:     if (found.length) docCandidates.push(...found);
 1151:   }
 1152: 
 1153:   if (!docCandidates.length) {
 1154:     const rawDate = String(date || "").trim();
 1155:     if (rawDate) {
 1156:       const { docs: found, error } = await fetchDrawDocsPreferUf({
 1157:         uf,
 1158:         extraWheres: [where("date", "==", rawDate)],
 1159:         policy: readPolicy,
 1160:       });
 1161:       if (error) throw error;
 1162:       if (found.length) docCandidates.push(...found);
 1163:     }
 1164:   }
 1165: 
 1166:   if (!docCandidates.length) {
 1167:     const { docs: found, error } = await fetchDrawDocsPreferUf({
 1168:       uf,
 1169:       extraWheres: [where("date", "==", ymdDate)],
 1170:       policy: readPolicy,
 1171:     });
 1172:     if (error) throw error;
 1173:     if (found.length) docCandidates.push(...found);
 1174:   }
 1175: 
 1176:   if (!docCandidates.length) {
 1177:     const brDate = ymdToBR(ymdDate);
 1178:     if (brDate) {
 1179:       const { docs: found, error } = await fetchDrawDocsPreferUf({
 1180:         uf,
 1181:         extraWheres: [where("date", "==", brDate)],
 1182:         policy: readPolicy,
 1183:       });
 1184:       if (error) throw error;
 1185:       if (found.length) docCandidates.push(...found);
 1186:     }
 1187:   }
 1188: 
 1189:   if (!docCandidates.length) return [];
 1190: 
 1191:   let baseAll = dedupeDrawsLocal(docCandidates.map(mapDrawDoc));
 1192:   baseAll = baseAll.filter((x) => (x.ymd || normalizeToYMD(x.date)) === ymdDate);
 1193: 
 1194:   const orderedAll = sortDrawsLocal(baseAll);
 1195: 
 1196:   const hydratedAll = await mapWithConcurrency(orderedAll, 6, async (item) => {
 1197:     const prizes = await fetchPrizesForDraw(item.drawId, positionsArr, item.prizes);
 1198:     const pc = Array.isArray(prizes) ? prizes.length : 0;
 1199:     return { ...item, prizes, prizesCount: pc, __mode: "detailed" };
 1200:   });
 1201: 
 1202:   let dayAll = dedupeDrawsLocal(hydratedAll);
 1203: 
 1204: if (scopeKey === FEDERAL_SCOPE_CODE) {
 1205:   dayAll = collapseFederalDailyCloneDraws(dayAll);
 1206: }
 1207: 
 1208:   const base = hourFilter?.kind
 1209:     ? dayAll.filter((d) => drawPassesHourFilter(d, hourFilter))
 1210:     : dayAll;
 1211: 
 1212:   let out = dedupeDrawsLocal(sortDrawsLocal(base));
 1213: 
 1214: if (scopeKey === FEDERAL_SCOPE_CODE) {
 1215:   out = collapseFederalDailyCloneDraws(out);
 1216: }
 1217: 
 1218:   if (allowMemoryCache) {
 1219:     cacheSet(DRAWS_CACHE, dayKey, out);
 1220:   }
 1221: 
 1222:   return out;
 1223: }
 1224: 
 1225: /* =========================
 1226:    API: Range
 1227: ========================= */
 1228: 
 1229: function drawsCacheKeyRange({
 1230:   scopeKey,
 1231:   from,
 1232:   to,
 1233:   positionsArr,
 1234:   hourFilter,
 1235:   mode,
 1236: }) {
 1237:   const p = positionsArr && positionsArr.length ? positionsArr.join(",") : "all";
 1238:   const h =
 1239:     hourFilter?.kind === "bucket"
 1240:       ? `bucket:${hourFilter.bucket}`
 1241:       : hourFilter?.kind === "exact"
 1242:       ? `exact:${hourFilter.hhmm}`
 1243:       : "all";
 1244:   const m = mode ? String(mode) : "detailed";
 1245:   return `range::${scopeKey}::${from}..${to}::pos=${p}::h=${h}::mode=${m}`;
 1246: }
 1247: 
 1248: function chooseRangeConcurrency(drawCount) {
 1249:   if (!Number.isFinite(drawCount)) return 6;
 1250:   if (drawCount <= 50) return 4;
 1251:   if (drawCount <= 300) return 6;
 1252:   if (drawCount <= 1200) return 8;
 1253:   return 10;
 1254: }
 1255: 
 1256: export function decideKingRangeMode({ dateFrom, dateTo, mode = "detailed" }) {
 1257:   const ymdFrom = normalizeToYMD(dateFrom);
 1258:   const ymdTo = normalizeToYMD(dateTo);
 1259:   if (!ymdFrom || !ymdTo) return "detailed";
 1260: 
 1261:   if (mode === "detailed" || mode === "aggregated") return mode;
 1262:   if (mode !== "auto") return "detailed";
 1263: 
 1264:   const days = daysBetweenInclusiveUTC(ymdFrom, ymdTo);
 1265:   if (!Number.isFinite(days) || days <= 0) return "detailed";
 1266:   return "detailed";
 1267: }
 1268: 
 1269: /**
 1270:  * ✅ Fallback barato (SEM prizes): usado quando falta índice composto no RANGE,
 1271:  * ou quando você quer aggregated sem custo de hidratar.
 1272:  */
 1273: async function fetchDrawsDayNoPrizes({ uf, dayYmd }) {
 1274:   const readPolicy = shouldForceFreshServerRead(dayYmd) ? "server" : DEFAULT_READ_POLICY;
 1275: 
 1276:   const { docs, error } = await fetchDrawDocsPreferUf({
 1277:     uf,
 1278:     extraWheres: [where("ymd", "==", dayYmd)],
 1279:     policy: readPolicy,
 1280:   });
 1281:   if (error) throw error;
 1282:   if (!docs || !docs.length) return [];
 1283: 
 1284:   let baseAll = dedupeDrawsLocal(docs.map(mapDrawDoc));
 1285:   baseAll = baseAll.filter((x) => (x.ymd || normalizeToYMD(x.date)) === dayYmd);
 1286: 
 1287:   const ordered = sortDrawsLocal(baseAll);
 1288: 
 1289:   return ordered.map((d) => {
 1290:     const embedded = normalizeEmbeddedPrizesForAggregated(d?.prizes);
 1291:     return {
 1292:       ...d,
 1293:       prizes: embedded,
 1294:       prizesCount: embedded.length || Number(d?.prizesCount || 0),
 1295:       __mode: "aggregated",
 1296:     };
 1297:   });
 1298: }
 1299: 
 1300: export async function getKingResultsByRange({
 1301:   uf,
 1302:   dateFrom,
 1303:   dateTo,
 1304:   closeHour = null,
 1305:   closeHourBucket = null,
 1306:   positions = null,
 1307:   mode = "detailed",
 1308:   readPolicy = DEFAULT_READ_POLICY,
 1309:   bypassCache = false,
 1310: }) {
 1311:   if (!uf || !dateFrom || !dateTo) {
 1312:     throw new Error("Parâmetros obrigatórios: uf, dateFrom, dateTo");
 1313:   }
 1314: 
 1315:   const scopeKey = canonicalScopeKey(uf);
 1316: 
 1317:   const positionsArr = normalizePositions(positions);
 1318: 
 1319:   const ymdFrom = normalizeToYMD(dateFrom);
 1320:   const ymdTo = normalizeToYMD(dateTo);
 1321:   if (!ymdFrom || !ymdTo) return [];
 1322: 
 1323:   const hourFilter = resolveHourFilter({ closeHour, closeHourBucket });
 1324: 
 1325:   const effectiveMode = decideKingRangeMode({
 1326:     dateFrom: ymdFrom,
 1327:     dateTo: ymdTo,
 1328:     mode,
 1329:   });
 1330: 
 1331:   const rangeKey = drawsCacheKeyRange({
 1332:     scopeKey,
 1333:     from: ymdFrom,
 1334:     to: ymdTo,
 1335:     positionsArr,
 1336:     hourFilter,
 1337:     mode: effectiveMode,
 1338:   });
 1339:   if (!bypassCache) {
 1340:     const cached = cacheGet(DRAWS_CACHE, rangeKey);
 1341:     if (cached) return cached;
 1342:   }
 1343: 
 1344:   const DOC_ID = documentId();
 1345:   const rangeOrder = [orderBy("ymd", "asc"), orderBy(DOC_ID)];
 1346: 
 1347:   const { docs, error, usedField } = await fetchDrawDocsPreferUf({
 1348:     uf,
 1349:     extraWheres: [where("ymd", ">=", ymdFrom), where("ymd", "<=", ymdTo)],
 1350:     extraOrderBy: rangeOrder,
 1351:     policy: readPolicy,
 1352:   });
 1353: 
 1354:   if (error) {
 1355:     if (isIndexError(error)) {
 1356:       const days = daysBetweenInclusiveUTC(ymdFrom, ymdTo);
 1357:       const MAX_FALLBACK_DAYS = 31;
 1358: 
 1359:       if (Number.isFinite(days) && days > 0 && days <= MAX_FALLBACK_DAYS) {
 1360:         if (effectiveMode === "aggregated") {
 1361:           const all = [];
 1362:           for (let i = 0; i < days; i += 1) {
 1363:             const day = addDaysUTC(ymdFrom, i);
 1364:             const dayDraws = await fetchDrawsDayNoPrizes({ uf, dayYmd: day });
 1365:             if (dayDraws.length) all.push(...dayDraws);
 1366:           }
 1367: 
 1368:           const baseAll = dedupeDrawsLocal(all);
 1369:           const base = hourFilter?.kind
 1370:             ? baseAll.filter((d) => drawPassesHourFilter(d, hourFilter))
 1371:             : baseAll;
 1372: 
 1373:           let out = dedupeDrawsLocal(sortDrawsLocal(base));
 1374: 
 1375:           if (!bypassCache) {
 1376:       cacheSet(DRAWS_CACHE, rangeKey, out);
 1377:     }
 1378:           return out;
 1379:         }
 1380: 
 1381:         const all = [];
 1382:         for (let i = 0; i < days; i += 1) {
 1383:           const day = addDaysUTC(ymdFrom, i);
 1384:           const dayDraws = await getKingResultsByDate({
 1385:             uf,
 1386:             date: day,
 1387:             closeHour,
 1388:             closeHourBucket,
 1389:             positions: positionsArr,
 1390:           });
 1391:           if (Array.isArray(dayDraws) && dayDraws.length) all.push(...dayDraws);
 1392:         }
 1393: 
 1394:         const baseAll = dedupeDrawsLocal(all);
 1395:         const base = hourFilter?.kind
 1396:           ? baseAll.filter((d) => drawPassesHourFilter(d, hourFilter))
 1397:           : baseAll;
 1398: 
 1399:         let out = dedupeDrawsLocal(sortDrawsLocal(base));
 1400: 
 1401:         if (!bypassCache) {
 1402:       cacheSet(DRAWS_CACHE, rangeKey, out);
 1403:     }
 1404:         return out;
 1405:       }
 1406: 
 1407:       const rawCode = String(error?.code || "");
 1408:       const rawMsg = String(error?.message || "");
 1409: 
 1410:       throw new Error(
 1411:         `Firestore: falta índice composto para RANGE (campo ${usedField} + ymd).\n` +
 1412:           `Crie/valide o índice no console (Collection: draws | Fields: ${usedField} ASC, ymd ASC, __name__ (documentId) ASC).\n` +
 1413:           `Obs: fallback automático só é aplicado para ranges até ${MAX_FALLBACK_DAYS} dias.\n` +
 1414:           (rawCode || rawMsg ? `Detalhe: ${rawCode} ${rawMsg}` : "")
 1415:       );
 1416:     }
 1417: 
 1418:     throw error;
 1419:   }
 1420: 
 1421:   if (!docs || !docs.length) {
 1422:     const days = daysBetweenInclusiveUTC(ymdFrom, ymdTo);
 1423:     const MAX_EMPTY_RANGE_FALLBACK_DAYS = 120;
 1424: 
 1425:     if (
 1426:       Number.isFinite(days) &&
 1427:       days > 0 &&
 1428:       days <= MAX_EMPTY_RANGE_FALLBACK_DAYS
 1429:     ) {
 1430:       if (effectiveMode === "aggregated") {
 1431:         const all = [];
 1432:         for (let i = 0; i < days; i += 1) {
 1433:           const day = addDaysUTC(ymdFrom, i);
 1434:           const dayDraws = await fetchDrawsDayNoPrizes({ uf, dayYmd: day });
 1435:           if (dayDraws.length) all.push(...dayDraws);
 1436:         }
 1437: 
 1438:         const baseAll = dedupeDrawsLocal(all);
 1439:         const base = hourFilter?.kind
 1440:           ? baseAll.filter((d) => drawPassesHourFilter(d, hourFilter))
 1441:           : baseAll;
 1442: 
 1443:         let out = dedupeDrawsLocal(sortDrawsLocal(base));
 1444: 
 1445:         if (!bypassCache) {
 1446:       cacheSet(DRAWS_CACHE, rangeKey, out);
 1447:     }
 1448:         return out;
 1449:       }
 1450: 
 1451:       const all = [];
 1452:       for (let i = 0; i < days; i += 1) {
 1453:         const day = addDaysUTC(ymdFrom, i);
 1454:         const dayDraws = await getKingResultsByDate({
 1455:           uf,
 1456:           date: day,
 1457:           closeHour,
 1458:           closeHourBucket,
 1459:           positions: positionsArr,
 1460:         });
 1461:         if (Array.isArray(dayDraws) && dayDraws.length) all.push(...dayDraws);
 1462:       }
 1463: 
 1464:       const baseAll = dedupeDrawsLocal(all);
 1465:       const base = hourFilter?.kind
 1466:         ? baseAll.filter((d) => drawPassesHourFilter(d, hourFilter))
 1467:         : baseAll;
 1468: 
 1469:       const ordered = sortDrawsLocal(base);
 1470: 
 1471:       const conc = chooseRangeConcurrency(ordered.length);
 1472:       const results = await mapWithConcurrency(ordered, conc, async (item) => {
 1473:         const prizes = await fetchPrizesForDraw(item.drawId, positionsArr, item.prizes);
 1474:         const pc = Array.isArray(prizes) ? prizes.length : 0;
 1475:         return { ...item, prizes, prizesCount: pc, __mode: "detailed" };
 1476:       });
 1477: 
 1478:       const out = dedupeDrawsLocal(results);
 1479:       if (!bypassCache) {
 1480:       cacheSet(DRAWS_CACHE, rangeKey, out);
 1481:     }
 1482:       return out;
 1483:     }
 1484: 
 1485:     return [];
 1486:   }
 1487: 
 1488:   const baseAll = dedupeDrawsLocal(docs.map(mapDrawDoc));
 1489:   const base = hourFilter?.kind
 1490:     ? baseAll.filter((d) => drawPassesHourFilter(d, hourFilter))
 1491:     : baseAll;
 1492: 
 1493:   const ordered = sortDrawsLocal(base);
 1494: 
 1495:   if (effectiveMode === "aggregated") {
 1496:     const out = dedupeDrawsLocal(
 1497:       ordered.map((d) => {
 1498:         const embedded = normalizeEmbeddedPrizesForAggregated(d?.prizes);
 1499:         return {
 1500:           ...d,
 1501:           prizes: embedded,
 1502:           prizesCount: Number.isFinite(Number(d?.prizesCount))
 1503:             ? Number(d.prizesCount)
 1504:             : embedded.length || 0,
 1505:           __mode: "aggregated",
 1506:         };
 1507:       })
 1508:     );
 1509:     if (!bypassCache) {
 1510:       cacheSet(DRAWS_CACHE, rangeKey, out);
 1511:     }
 1512:     return out;
 1513:   }
 1514: 
 1515:   const conc = chooseRangeConcurrency(ordered.length);
 1516: 
 1517:   const results = await mapWithConcurrency(ordered, conc, async (item) => {
 1518:     const prizes = await fetchPrizesForDraw(item.drawId, positionsArr, item.prizes);
 1519:     const pc = Array.isArray(prizes) ? prizes.length : 0;
 1520:     return { ...item, prizes, prizesCount: pc, __mode: "detailed" };
 1521:   });
 1522: 
 1523:   const out = dedupeDrawsLocal(results);
 1524:   if (!bypassCache) {
 1525:       cacheSet(DRAWS_CACHE, rangeKey, out);
 1526:     }
 1527:   return out;
 1528: }
 1529: 
 1530: /* =========================
 1531:    ✅ Hidratação de prizes (para UX no "Todos")
 1532: ========================= */
 1533: 
 1534: export async function hydrateKingDrawsWithPrizes({
 1535:   draws,
 1536:   positions = null,
 1537:   concurrency = null,
 1538: }) {
 1539:   const arr = Array.isArray(draws) ? draws : [];
 1540:   if (!arr.length) return [];
 1541: 
 1542:   const positionsArr = normalizePositions(positions);
 1543: 
 1544:   const conc =
 1545:     Number.isFinite(Number(concurrency)) && Number(concurrency) > 0
 1546:       ? Number(concurrency)
 1547:       : chooseRangeConcurrency(arr.length);
 1548: 
 1549:   const results = await mapWithConcurrency(arr, conc, async (item) => {
 1550:     const prizes = await fetchPrizesForDraw(item.drawId, positionsArr, item.prizes);
 1551:     const pc = Array.isArray(prizes) ? prizes.length : 0;
 1552:     return { ...item, prizes, prizesCount: pc, __mode: "detailed" };
 1553:   });
 1554: 
 1555:   return dedupeDrawsLocal(results);
 1556: }
 1557: 
 1558: /* =========================
 1559:    ✅ ATRASADOS (Late) — 100% baseado na SUA base
 1560: ========================= */
 1561: 
 1562: function normalizeLotteriesList(lotteries) {
 1563:   const arr = Array.isArray(lotteries) ? lotteries : [];
 1564:   const cleaned = arr
 1565:     .map((x) => String(x ?? "").trim().toUpperCase())
 1566:     .filter(Boolean);
 1567:   return cleaned.length ? Array.from(new Set(cleaned)) : null;
 1568: }
 1569: 
 1570: function drawPassesLotteriesFilter(draw, lotteriesArr) {
 1571:   if (!lotteriesArr || !lotteriesArr.length) return true;
 1572:   const code = String(draw?.lottery_code ?? "").trim().toUpperCase();
 1573:   if (!code) return false;
 1574:   return lotteriesArr.includes(code);
 1575: }
 1576: 
 1577: function lateCacheKey({
 1578:   scopeKey,
 1579:   fromYmd,
 1580:   toYmd,
 1581:   baseYmd,
 1582:   prizePosition,
 1583:   hourFilter,
 1584:   lotteriesArr,
 1585:   chunkDays,
 1586: }) {
 1587:   const h =
 1588:     hourFilter?.kind === "bucket"
 1589:       ? `bucket:${hourFilter.bucket}`
 1590:       : hourFilter?.kind === "exact"
 1591:       ? `exact:${hourFilter.hhmm}`
 1592:       : "all";
 1593:   const lots = lotteriesArr && lotteriesArr.length ? lotteriesArr.join(",") : "all";
 1594:   const cd = Number(chunkDays) || 15;
 1595:   const pos = Number(prizePosition) || 1;
 1596:   return `late::${scopeKey}::${fromYmd}..${toYmd}::base=${baseYmd}::pos=${pos}::h=${h}::lots=${lots}::chunk=${cd}`;
 1597: }
 1598: 
 1599: function hourToNumSafe(hhmm) {
 1600:   const s = normalizeHourLike(hhmm);
 1601:   const m = s.match(/^(\d{2}):(\d{2})$/);
 1602:   if (!m) return Number.POSITIVE_INFINITY;
 1603:   return Number(m[1]) * 100 + Number(m[2]);
 1604: }
 1605: 
 1606: export async function getKingLateByRange({
 1607:   uf,
 1608:   dateFrom,
 1609:   dateTo,
 1610:   baseDate = null,
 1611:   lotteries = null,
 1612:   prizePosition = 1,
 1613:   closeHour = null,
 1614:   closeHourBucket = null,
 1615:   chunkDays = 15,
 1616: }) {
 1617:   if (!uf || !dateFrom || !dateTo) {
 1618:     throw new Error("Parâmetros obrigatórios: uf, dateFrom, dateTo");
 1619:   }
 1620: 
 1621:   const scopeKey = canonicalScopeKey(uf);
 1622: 
 1623:   const fromYmd = normalizeToYMD(dateFrom);
 1624:   const toYmd = normalizeToYMD(dateTo);
 1625:   if (!fromYmd || !toYmd) return [];
 1626: 
 1627:   const baseYmd = normalizeToYMD(baseDate) || toYmd;
 1628: 
 1629:   const hourFilter = resolveHourFilter({ closeHour, closeHourBucket });
 1630:   const lotteriesArr = normalizeLotteriesList(lotteries);
 1631: 
 1632:   const cacheKey = lateCacheKey({
 1633:     scopeKey,
 1634:     fromYmd,
 1635:     toYmd,
 1636:     baseYmd,
 1637:     prizePosition,
 1638:     hourFilter,
 1639:     lotteriesArr,
 1640:     chunkDays,
 1641:   });
 1642: 
 1643:   const cached = cacheGet(LATE_CACHE, cacheKey);
 1644:   if (cached) return cached;
 1645: 
 1646:   const posNum = Number(prizePosition) || 1;
 1647: 
 1648:   const lastSeen = new Map();
 1649: 
 1650:   let cursorTo = toYmd;
 1651:   const chunk = Math.max(3, Math.min(60, Number(chunkDays) || 15));
 1652: 
 1653:   if (cursorTo < fromYmd) {
 1654:     const outEmpty = Array.from({ length: 25 }, (_, i) => ({
 1655:       pos: i + 1,
 1656:       grupo: i + 1,
 1657:       lastYmd: null,
 1658:       lastHour: null,
 1659:       lastDrawId: null,
 1660:       lastLottery: null,
 1661:       atrasoDias: null,
 1662:     }));
 1663:     cacheSet(LATE_CACHE, cacheKey, outEmpty);
 1664:     return outEmpty;
 1665:   }
 1666: 
 1667:   while (cursorTo >= fromYmd && lastSeen.size < 25) {
 1668:     let chunkFrom = addDaysUTC(cursorTo, -(chunk - 1));
 1669:     if (chunkFrom < fromYmd) chunkFrom = fromYmd;
 1670: 
 1671:     const draws = await getKingResultsByRange({
 1672:       uf,
 1673:       dateFrom: chunkFrom,
 1674:       dateTo: cursorTo,
 1675:       closeHour,
 1676:       closeHourBucket,
 1677:       positions: [posNum],
 1678:       mode: "detailed",
 1679:     });
 1680: 
 1681:     const desc = sortDrawsLocalDesc(dedupeDrawsLocal(draws));
 1682: 
 1683:     for (const d of desc) {
 1684:       if (!d) continue;
 1685: 
 1686:       if (hourFilter?.kind && !drawPassesHourFilter(d, hourFilter)) continue;
 1687:       if (lotteriesArr && !drawPassesLotteriesFilter(d, lotteriesArr)) continue;
 1688: 
 1689:       const ymd = d.ymd || normalizeToYMD(d.date);
 1690:       if (!ymd || !isYMD(ymd)) continue;
 1691: 
 1692:       const p = Array.isArray(d.prizes)
 1693:         ? d.prizes.find(
 1694:             (x) => Number(x?.position) === posNum && isValidGrupo(x?.grupo)
 1695:           )
 1696:         : null;
 1697: 
 1698:       const g = p?.grupo;
 1699:       if (!isValidGrupo(g)) continue;
 1700: 
 1701:       if (!lastSeen.has(g)) {
 1702:         lastSeen.set(g, {
 1703:           ymd,
 1704:           hour: d.close_hour || d.closeHour || "",
 1705:           drawId: d.drawId || d.id || null,
 1706:           lottery_code: d.lottery_code || null,
 1707:         });
 1708: 
 1709:         if (lastSeen.size >= 25) break;
 1710:       }
 1711:     }
 1712: 
 1713:     cursorTo = addDaysUTC(chunkFrom, -1);
 1714:   }
 1715: 
 1716:   const rows = [];
 1717:   for (let g = 1; g <= 25; g += 1) {
 1718:     const seen = lastSeen.get(g) || null;
 1719:     const lastYmd = seen?.ymd || null;
 1720:     const diff = lastYmd ? daysDiffUTC(lastYmd, baseYmd) : NaN;
 1721:     const atrasoDias =
 1722:       lastYmd && Number.isFinite(diff) ? Math.max(0, diff) : null;
 1723: 
 1724:     rows.push({
 1725:       pos: 0,
 1726:       grupo: g,
 1727:       lastYmd,
 1728:       lastHour: seen?.hour ? normalizeHourLike(seen.hour) : null,
 1729:       lastDrawId: seen?.drawId || null,
 1730:       lastLottery: seen?.lottery_code || null,
 1731:       atrasoDias,
 1732:     });
 1733:   }
 1734: 
 1735:   const sorted = [...rows].sort((a, b) => {
 1736:     const aa = Number.isFinite(Number(a?.atrasoDias)) ? Number(a.atrasoDias) : -1;
 1737:     const bb = Number.isFinite(Number(b?.atrasoDias)) ? Number(b.atrasoDias) : -1;
 1738:     if (bb !== aa) return bb - aa;
 1739: 
 1740:     const ha = hourToNumSafe(a?.lastHour);
 1741:     const hb = hourToNumSafe(b?.lastHour);
 1742:     if (ha !== hb) return ha - hb;
 1743: 
 1744:     return Number(a?.grupo || 0) - Number(b?.grupo || 0);
 1745:   });
 1746: 
 1747:   const out = sorted.map((r, idx) => ({ ...r, pos: idx + 1 }));
 1748: 
 1749:   cacheSet(LATE_CACHE, cacheKey, out);
 1750:   return out;
 1751: }

    4: /**
    5:  * FiltersBar (Premium)
    6:  *
    7:  * ✅ Loteria:
    8:  * - filters.loteria: "PT_RIO" | "FEDERAL" | "LOOK" | "NACIONAL"
    9:  *   (default: "PT_RIO")
   10:  *
   11:  * ✅ Horário (domínio próprio por loteria):
   12:  * - PT_RIO: Todos + 09h/11h/14h/16h/18h/21h
   13:  * - FEDERAL: Todos + 19h/20h
   14:  * - LOOK: Todos + 07h/09h/11h/14h/16h/18h/21h/23h
   15:  * - NACIONAL: Todos + 02h/08h/10h/12h/15h/17h/21h/23h
   16:  *
   17:  * ✅ Segurança:
   18:  * - Ao trocar loteria, reseta filtros sensíveis:
   19:  *   horario -> "Todos"
   20:  *   animal  -> "Todos"
   21:  *   posicao -> "Todos"
   22:  *
   23:  * posicaoMode:
   24:  * - "full" (default): Todos + 1º..7º
   25:  * - "v1": trava em 1º (desabilita select e lista só 1º)
   26:  *
   27:  * posicoesFromOptionsMode:
   28:  * - "ignore" (default): IGNORA options.posicoes (posição é domínio fixo)
   29:  * - "respect": respeita options.posicoes se vier (com fallback seguro)
   30:  *
   31:  * Extras (freeze-safe):
   32:  * - disabledAll: trava todos selects (útil para modo DEMO)
   33:  * - onBlocked: callback quando usuário tenta interagir enquanto bloqueado
   34:  */
   35: 
   36: function normalizeValue(v, fallback = "Todos") {
   37:   const s = String(v ?? "").trim();
   38:   return s ? s : fallback;
   39: }
   40: 
   41: function sortPTBR(a, b) {
   42:   return String(a).localeCompare(String(b), "pt-BR", { sensitivity: "base" });
   43: }
   44: 
   45: function normalizeLoteriaInput(v) {
   46:   const raw = String(v ?? "").trim();
   47:   if (!raw) return "PT_RIO";
   48: 
   49:   const key = raw
   50:     .toLowerCase()
   51:     .normalize("NFD")
   52:     .replace(/[\u0300-\u036f]/g, "")
   53:     .replace(/\s+/g, " ")
   54:     .trim();
   55: 
   56:   if (key === "federal" || key === "fed" || key === "br" || key === "brasil")
   57:     return "FEDERAL";
   58:   if (key === "rj" || key === "rio" || key === "pt_rio" || key === "pt-rio")
   59:     return "PT_RIO";
   60: 
   61:   const out = key
   62:     .toUpperCase()
   63:     .replace(/[^A-Z0-9]+/g, "_")
   64:     .replace(/^_+|_+$/g, "");
   65: 
   66:   return out || "PT_RIO";
   67: }
   68: 
   69: /** Normaliza posição para "1º..7º" ou "Todos" */
   70: function normalizePosicaoInput(v) {
   71:   const s = String(v ?? "").trim();
   72:   if (!s) return "Todos";
   73:   if (s.toLowerCase() === "todos") return "Todos";
   74: 
   75:   const mNum = s.match(/^(\d{1,2})$/);
   76:   if (mNum) return `${Number(mNum[1])}º`;
   77: 
   78:   const mOrd = s.match(/^(\d{1,2})\s*º$/);
   79:   if (mOrd) return `${Number(mOrd[1])}º`;
   80: 
   81:   return s;
   82: }
   83: 
   84: /** ✅ Normaliza Dia da Semana para o formato curto: Dom/Seg/Ter/Qua/Qui/Sex/Sáb/Todos */
   85: function normalizeDiaSemanaInput(v) {
   86:   const raw = String(v ?? "").trim();
   87:   if (!raw) return "Todos";
   88: 
   89:   const key = raw
   90:     .toLowerCase()
   91:     .normalize("NFD")
   92:     .replace(/[\u0300-\u036f]/g, "")
   93:     .replace(/\s+/g, " ")
   94:     .trim();
   95: 
   96:   if (key === "todos") return "Todos";
   97: 
   98:   const map = {
   99:     dom: "Dom",
  100:     domingo: "Dom",
  101:     seg: "Seg",
  102:     "segunda-feira": "Seg",
  103:     "segunda feira": "Seg",
  104:     segunda: "Seg",
  105:     ter: "Ter",
  106:     "terca-feira": "Ter",
  107:     "terca feira": "Ter",
  108:     terca: "Ter",
  109:     qua: "Qua",
  110:     "quarta-feira": "Qua",
  111:     "quarta feira": "Qua",
  112:     quarta: "Qua",
  113:     qui: "Qui",
  114:     "quinta-feira": "Qui",
  115:     "quinta feira": "Qui",
  116:     quinta: "Qui",
  117:     sex: "Sex",
  118:     "sexta-feira": "Sex",
  119:     "sexta feira": "Sex",
  120:     sexta: "Sex",
  121:     sab: "Sáb",
  122:     sabado: "Sáb",
  123:     "sabado-feira": "Sáb",
  124:     "sabado feira": "Sáb",
  125:     sabb: "Sáb",
  126:     sáb: "Sáb",
  127:     sábado: "Sáb",
  128:   };
  129: 
  130:   return map[key] || raw;
  131: }
  132: 
  133: /** Garante que o value exista na lista */
  134: function ensureValueInList({
  135:   value,
  136:   list,
  137:   forceValue = null,
  138:   fallback = "Todos",
  139:   normalizeFn = null,
  140: }) {
  141:   const base = forceValue ?? value;
  142:   const v0 = normalizeValue(base, fallback);
  143:   const v = typeof normalizeFn === "function" ? normalizeFn(v0) : v0;
  144: 
  145:   const arr = Array.isArray(list) ? list : [];
  146:   if (!arr.length) return v;
  147: 
  148:   const isObjList = typeof arr[0] === "object" && arr[0] && "value" in arr[0];
  149: 
  150:   const norm = (x) => {
  151:     const s = normalizeValue(x, "");
  152:     return typeof normalizeFn === "function" ? normalizeFn(s) : s;
  153:   };
  154: 
  155:   if (isObjList) {
  156:     const values = new Set(arr.map((x) => norm(x?.value)));
  157:     if (values.has(v)) return v;
  158:     if (values.has("Todos")) return "Todos";
  159:     const first = norm(arr[0]?.value);
  160:     return first || fallback;
  161:   }
  162: 
  163:   const values = new Set(arr.map((x) => norm(x)));
  164:   if (values.has(v)) return v;
  165:   if (values.has("Todos")) return "Todos";
  166: 
  167:   const first = norm(arr[0]);
  168:   return first || fallback;
  169: }

  573:     (name, next) => {
  574:       if (disabledAll) {
  575:         if (typeof onBlocked === "function") onBlocked(name);
  576:         return;
  577:       }

  579:       // ✅ Troca loteria: normaliza e reseta filtros sensíveis SEMPRE
  580:       if (name === "loteria") {
  581:         const nextLot = normalizeLoteriaInput(next);
  582:         onChange("loteria", nextLot);
  583: 
  584:         // reset para evitar “estatística inválida” por estado anterior
  585:         onChange("horario", "Todos");
  586:         onChange("animal", "Todos");
  587:         onChange("posicao", "Todos");
  588: 
  589:         return;
  590:       }
  591: 
  592:       if (lockPosicaoV1 && name === "posicao") {
  593:         onChange("posicao", "1º");
  594:         return;
  595:       }
  596: 
  597:       if (name === "posicao") {
  598:         onChange(name, normalizePosicaoInput(next));
  599:         return;
  600:       }
  601: 
  602:       if (name === "diaSemana") {
  603:         onChange(name, normalizeDiaSemanaInput(next));
  604:         return;
  605:       }
  606: 
  607:       onChange(name, next);
  608:     },
  609:     [disabledAll, onBlocked, lockPosicaoV1, onChange]
  610:   );
  611: 
  612:   const Item = ({
  613:     label,
  614:     value,
  615:     name,
  616:     list,
  617:     wrapClassName,
  618:     wrapStyle,
  619:     disabled = false,
  620:     forceValue = null,
  621:   }) => {
  622:     const normalizeFn =
  623:       name === "posicao"
  624:         ? normalizePosicaoInput
  625:         : name === "diaSemana"
  626:         ? normalizeDiaSemanaInput
  627:         : name === "loteria"
  628:         ? normalizeLoteriaInput
  629:         : null;
  630: 
  631:     const normalizedValue = ensureValueInList({
  632:       value,
  633:       list,
  634:       forceValue,
  635:       fallback: name === "loteria" ? "PT_RIO" : "Todos",
  636:       normalizeFn,
  637:     });
  638: 
  639:     const isObjList =
  640:       Array.isArray(list) && list.length && typeof list[0] === "object" && list[0];
  641: 
  642:     const finalDisabled = !!disabledAll || !!disabled;
  643: 
  644:     const block = (e) => {
  645:       if (!finalDisabled) return;
  646:       try {
  647:         e?.preventDefault?.();
  648:         e?.stopPropagation?.();
  649:       } catch {}
  650:       if (typeof onBlocked === "function") onBlocked(name);
  651:     };
  652: 
  653:     return (
  654:       <div className={wrapClassName} style={{ ...wrapStyle, minWidth: 0 }}>
  655:         <div style={ui.item}>
  656:           <div style={ui.label} title={label}>
  657:             {label}
  658:           </div>
  659: 
  660:           <div style={ui.selectWrap}>
  661:             <select
  662:               value={normalizedValue}
  663:               aria-disabled={finalDisabled ? "true" : "false"}
  664:               tabIndex={finalDisabled ? -1 : 0}   // ✅ FIX: evita foco via TAB quando bloqueado
  665:               onWheelCapture={block}              // ✅ FIX: bloqueia troca por roda do mouse
  666:               onMouseDownCapture={block}
  667:               onClickCapture={block}
  668:               onKeyDownCapture={(e) => {
  669:                 if (!finalDisabled) return;
  670:                 block(e);
  671:               }}
  672:               onChange={(e) => handleChange(name, e.target.value)}
  673:               style={{
  674:                 ...ui.select,
  675:                 ...(finalDisabled ? ui.selectDisabled : null),
  676:               }}
  677:             >
  678:               {isObjList
  679:                 ? list.map((opt, idx) => (
  680:                     <option key={`${name}_${String(opt?.value)}_${idx}`} value={opt?.value}>
  681:                       {opt?.label}
  682:                     </option>
  683:                   ))
  684:                 : (list || ["Todos"]).map((opt, idx) => (
  685:                     <option key={`${name}_${String(opt)}_${idx}`} value={opt}>
  686:                       {opt}
  687:                     </option>
  688:                   ))}
  689:             </select>
  690: 
  691:             <span aria-hidden="true" style={ui.caret} />
  692:           </div>
  693:         </div>
  694:       </div>
  695:     );
  696:   };
  697: 
  698:   // FEDERAL NÃO trava horário — só troca a lista (Todos/19h/20h)
  699:   const horarioForcedValue = null;
  700:   const horarioDisabled = false;
  701: 
  702:   return (
  703:     <div style={ui.bar}>
  704:       <style>{ui.styleTag}</style>
  705: 
  706:       <div className="pp_filters_grid" style={ui.grid}>
  707:         <Item
  708:           label="Loteria"
  709:           name="loteria"
  710:           value={loteria}
  711:           list={defaultOptions.loterias}
  712:           wrapClassName="pp_f_loteria"
  713:           wrapStyle={ui.spanLoteria}
  714:         />
  715:

  717:           label="Mês"

  719:           value={filters.mes}
  720:           list={defaultOptions.meses}
  721:           wrapClassName="pp_f_mes"

  723:         />

    1: // src/pages/Dashboard/components/LeftRankingTable.jsx
    2: import React, { useMemo, useState } from "react";

    4: import {
    5:   getAnimalLabel as getAnimalLabelFn,

    7:   guessPalpiteFromGrupo as guessPalpiteFromGrupoFn,
    8: } from "../../../constants/bichoMap";
    9: 
   10: /**
   11:  * LeftRankingTable
   12:  * - Ordenável: Grupo | Animal | Apar.
   13:  * - Não ordenável: Imagem | Palpite
   14:  *
   15:  * ✅ SEM SCROLL (nem vertical nem horizontal) e sem cortar:
   16:  * - Body sem overflow
   17:  * - Layout compactado para caber 25 linhas
   18:  *
   19:  * ✅ DEMO safe:
   20:  * - prop `disabled`: bloqueia ordenação e seleção
   21:  *
   22:  * ✅ Ajuste desta versão:
   23:  * - Aumenta um pouco a legibilidade (textos/números)
   24:  * - Aumenta um pouco as imagens
   25:  * - Mantém 25 linhas sem scroll
   26:  */
   27: 
   28: function digitsOnly(v) {
   29:   const s = String(v ?? "").trim();
   30:   if (!s) return "";
   31:   return s.replace(/\D+/g, "");
   32: }
   33: 
   34: function toMilhar4(v) {
   35:   const d = digitsOnly(v);
   36:   if (!d) return null;
   37:   return d.length >= 4 ? d.slice(-4).padStart(4, "0") : d.padStart(4, "0");
   38: }
   39: 
   40: function safeNumber(n) {
   41:   const x = Number(n);
   42:   return Number.isFinite(x) ? x : 0;
   43: }
   44: 
   45: function pickAparCount(r) {
   46:   return safeNumber(
   47:     r?.total ??
   48:       r?.apar ??
   49:       r?.aparicoes ??
   50:       r?.aparições ??
   51:       r?.count ??
   52:       r?.value ??
   53:       0
   54:   );
   55: }
   56: 
   57: function fmtIntPT(n) {
   58:   const x = safeNumber(n);
   59:   try {
   60:     return new Intl.NumberFormat("pt-BR", { maximumFractionDigits: 0 }).format(x);
   61:   } catch {
   62:     return String(x);
   63:   }
   64: }
   65: 
   66: function normalizeGrupoNumber(v) {
   67:   const s = String(v ?? "").trim();
   68:   if (!s) return 0;
   69:   const n = Number(s);
   70:   return Number.isFinite(n) ? n : 0;
   71: }
   72: 
   73: function grupoTo2(g) {
   74:   const n = normalizeGrupoNumber(g);
   75:   return String(n).padStart(2, "0");
   76: }
   77: 
   78: function uniq(arr) {
   79:   return Array.from(new Set(arr.filter(Boolean)));
   80: }
   81: 
   82: function safeStr(v) {
   83:   return String(v ?? "").trim();
   84: }
   85: 
   86: /** ✅ Gera candidatos de imagem (robusto) */
   87: function buildImgCandidates(getImgFromGrupo, grupo) {
   88:   const base96 = safeStr(getImgFromGrupo(grupo, 96));
   89:   const base128 = safeStr(getImgFromGrupo(grupo, 128));
   90:   const base64 = safeStr(getImgFromGrupo(grupo, 64));
   91: 
   92:   const bases = [base128, base96, base64].filter(Boolean);
   93: 
   94:   const out = [];
   95:   for (const src of bases) {
   96:     out.push(src);
   97: 
   98:     if (/\.png(\?|#|$)/i.test(src)) {
   99:       out.push(src.replace(/\.png(\?|#|$)/i, ".jpg$1"));
  100:       out.push(src.replace(/\.png(\?|#|$)/i, ".jpeg$1"));
  101:     }
  102: 
  103:     if (/\.jpe?g(\?|#|$)/i.test(src)) {
  104:       out.push(src.replace(/\.jpe?g(\?|#|$)/i, ".png$1"));
  105:     }
  106:   }
  107: 
  108:   return uniq(out);
  109: }
  110: 
  111: export default function LeftRankingTable({
  112:   locationLabel = "Rio de Janeiro, RJ, Brasil",
  113:   loading = false,
  114:   error = null,
  115:   data = [],
  116:   selectedGrupo = null,
  117:   onSelectGrupo = null,
  118: 
  119:   palpitesByGrupo = {},
  120:   aparGlobalByGrupo = null,
  121:   fillMissingGroups = false,
  122: 
  123:   // ✅ trava ações no DEMO
  124:   disabled = false,
  125: }) {
  126:   const [sort, setSort] = useState({ key: "apar", dir: "desc" });
  127: 
  128:   const getAnimalLabel = useMemo(() => {
  129:     return typeof getAnimalLabelFn === "function"
  130:       ? getAnimalLabelFn
  131:       : ({ animal }) => String(animal || "");
  132:   }, []);
  133: 
  134:   const getImgFromGrupo = useMemo(() => {
  135:     return typeof getImgFromGrupoFn === "function" ? getImgFromGrupoFn : () => "";
  136:   }, []);
  137: 
  138:   const guessPalpiteFromGrupo = useMemo(() => {
  139:     return typeof guessPalpiteFromGrupoFn === "function"
  140:       ? guessPalpiteFromGrupoFn
  141:       : () => "----";
  142:   }, []);
  143: 
  144:   const rows = useMemo(() => {
  145:     const arr = Array.isArray(data) ? data : [];
  146:     const hasGlobal = aparGlobalByGrupo && typeof aparGlobalByGrupo === "object";
  147: 
  148:     const byGrupo2 = new Map();
  149:     for (const r of arr) {
  150:       const grupo = normalizeGrupoNumber(r?.grupo);
  151:       if (!Number.isFinite(grupo) || grupo <= 0) continue;
  152:       const g2 = grupoTo2(grupo);
  153: 
  154:       const prev = byGrupo2.get(g2);
  155:       if (!prev) {
  156:         byGrupo2.set(g2, r);
  157:         continue;
  158:       }
  159: 
  160:       const prevA = pickAparCount(prev);
  161:       const curA = pickAparCount(r);
  162:       if (curA >= prevA) byGrupo2.set(g2, r);
  163:     }
  164: 
  165:     let gruposBase = [];
  166: 
  167:     if (fillMissingGroups) {
  168:       gruposBase = Array.from({ length: 25 }, (_, i) => i + 1);
  169:     } else {
  170:       const keys = Array.from(byGrupo2.keys());
  171:       if (!keys.length) return [];
  172:       gruposBase = keys
  173:         .map((g2) => Number(g2))
  174:         .filter((n) => Number.isFinite(n) && n >= 1 && n <= 25)
  175:         .sort((a, b) => a - b);
  176:     }
  177: 
  178:     const out = [];
  179:     for (const g of gruposBase) {
  180:       const grupo = Number(g);
  181:       const grupo2 = grupoTo2(grupo);
  182:       const srcRow = byGrupo2.get(grupo2) || null;
  183: 
  184:       const animalRaw =
  185:         (srcRow?.animal ?? srcRow?.label ?? srcRow?.animalLabel ?? "") || "";
  186: 
  187:       let animalLabel = "";
  188:       try {
  189:         animalLabel = getAnimalLabel({ grupo, animal: animalRaw }) || "";
  190:       } catch {
  191:         animalLabel = "";
  192:       }
  193:       animalLabel = String(animalLabel || animalRaw || "").trim();
  194: 
  195:       const palpiteFromByGrupo =
  196:         palpitesByGrupo && typeof palpitesByGrupo === "object"
  197:           ? toMilhar4(palpitesByGrupo[grupo2])
  198:           : null;
  199: 
  200:       const palpiteFromRow =
  201:         toMilhar4(
  202:           srcRow?.palpite ??
  203:             srcRow?.palpite4 ??
  204:             srcRow?.milhar ??
  205:             srcRow?.milhar4 ??
  206:             srcRow?.palpiteMilhar ??
  207:             srcRow?.palpite_milhar ??
  208:             srcRow?.p ??
  209:             null
  210:         ) || null;
  211: 
  212:       const palpiteFallback = toMilhar4(guessPalpiteFromGrupo(grupo)) || "----";
  213:       const palpite = palpiteFromByGrupo || palpiteFromRow || palpiteFallback;
  214: 
  215:       const aparFromGlobal = hasGlobal ? safeNumber(aparGlobalByGrupo[grupo2]) : NaN;
  216:       const apar = Number.isFinite(aparFromGlobal) ? aparFromGlobal : pickAparCount(srcRow);
  217: 
  218:       const imgCandidates = buildImgCandidates(getImgFromGrupo, grupo);
  219: 
  220:       out.push({
  221:         grupo,
  222:         grupo2,
  223:         animalRaw,
  224:         animalLabel,
  225:         apar,
  226:         palpite,
  227:         imgCandidates,
  228:       });
  229:     }
  230: 
  231:     return out;
  232:   }, [
  233:     data,
  234:     getAnimalLabel,
  235:     getImgFromGrupo,
  236:     guessPalpiteFromGrupo,
  237:     palpitesByGrupo,
  238:     aparGlobalByGrupo,
  239:     fillMissingGroups,
  240:   ]);
  241: 
  242:   const sortedRows = useMemo(() => {
  243:     const list = Array.isArray(rows) ? [...rows] : [];
  244:     if (!list.length) return list;
  245: 
  246:     const dirMul = sort.dir === "asc" ? 1 : -1;
  247: 
  248:     const cmpText = (a, b) =>
  249:       String(a || "").localeCompare(String(b || ""), "pt-BR", {
  250:         sensitivity: "base",
  251:       });
  252: 
  253:     list.sort((A, B) => {
  254:       let vA;
  255:       let vB;
  256: 
  257:       switch (sort.key) {
  258:         case "grupo":
  259:           vA = Number(A.grupo || 0);
  260:           vB = Number(B.grupo || 0);
  261:           if (vA === vB) return 0;
  262:           return (vA - vB) * dirMul;
  263: 
  264:         case "animal":
  265:           vA = String(A.animalLabel || A.animalRaw || "");
  266:           vB = String(B.animalLabel || B.animalRaw || "");
  267:           {
  268:             const c = cmpText(vA, vB) * dirMul;
  269:             if (c !== 0) return c;
  270:             return (Number(A.grupo || 0) - Number(B.grupo || 0)) * 1;
  271:           }
  272: 
  273:         case "apar":
  274:         default:
  275:           vA = Number(A.apar || 0);
  276:           vB = Number(B.apar || 0);
  277:           {
  278:             const c = (vA - vB) * dirMul;
  279:             if (c !== 0) return c;
  280:             return (Number(A.grupo || 0) - Number(B.grupo || 0)) * 1;
  281:           }
  282:       }
  283:     });
  284: 
  285:     return list;
  286:   }, [rows, sort.key, sort.dir]);
  287: 
  288:   const formatGrupo2 = (n) => String(Number(n || 0)).padStart(2, "0");
  289: 
  290:   const toggleSort = (key) => {
  291:     if (disabled) return;
  292:     if (!["grupo", "animal", "apar"].includes(key)) return;
  293: 
  294:     setSort((prev) => {
  295:       if (prev.key === key) {
  296:         return { key, dir: prev.dir === "asc" ? "desc" : "asc" };
  297:       }
  298:       const defaultDir = key === "animal" ? "asc" : "desc";
  299:       return { key, dir: defaultDir };
  300:     });
  301:   };
  302: 
  303:   const sortArrow = (key) => {
  304:     if (sort.key === key) return sort.dir === "asc" ? "▲" : "▼";
  305:     return "▼";
  306:   };
  307: 
  308:   const arrowOpacity = (key) => (sort.key === key ? 1 : 0.28);
  309: 
  310:   const hasSelection =
  311:     Number.isFinite(Number(selectedGrupo)) &&
  312:     Number(selectedGrupo) >= 1 &&
  313:     Number(selectedGrupo) <= 25;
  314: 
  315:   const canSelect = !disabled && typeof onSelectGrupo === "function";
  316: 
  317:   const handleSelectGrupo = (grupo) => {
  318:     if (!canSelect) return;
  319:     const g = Number(grupo);
  320:     const cur = Number(selectedGrupo);
  321:     if (Number.isFinite(cur) && cur === g) {
  322:       onSelectGrupo(null);
  323:       return;
  324:     }
  325:     onSelectGrupo(g);
  326:   };
  327: 
  328:   const renderBody = () => {
  329:     if (loading) {
  330:       return (
  331:         <div style={ui.emptyWrap}>
  332:           <div style={ui.emptyTitle}>Carregando…</div>
  333:           <div style={ui.emptyHint}>Buscando sorteios na base.</div>
  334:         </div>
  335:       );
  336:     }
  337: 
  338:     if (error) {
  339:       return (
  340:         <div style={ui.emptyWrap}>
  341:           <div style={ui.emptyTitle}>Erro ao carregar</div>
  342:           <div style={ui.emptyHint}>
  343:             {String(error?.message || error || "Erro desconhecido")}
  344:           </div>
  345:         </div>
  346:       );
  347:     }
  348: 
  349:     if (!sortedRows.length) {
  350:       return (
  351:         <div style={ui.emptyWrap}>
  352:           <div style={ui.emptyTitle}>Sem dados</div>
  353:           <div style={ui.emptyHint}>
  354:             Nenhum registro disponível para o filtro/período atual.
  355:           </div>
  356:         </div>
  357:       );
  358:     }
  359: 
  360:     return (
  361:       <div className="pp_left_body" style={ui.bodyNoScroll}>
  362:         {sortedRows.map((r) => {
  363:           const isSel = Number(selectedGrupo) === Number(r.grupo);
  364:           const dim = hasSelection && !isSel;
  365: 
  366:           const initialSrc =
  367:             Array.isArray(r.imgCandidates) && r.imgCandidates.length
  368:               ? r.imgCandidates[0]
  369:               : "";
  370: 
  371:           return (
  372:             <div
  373:               key={`g_${formatGrupo2(r.grupo)}`}
  374:               data-dim={dim ? "1" : "0"}
  375:               aria-disabled={disabled ? "true" : "false"}
  376:               style={{
  377:                 ...ui.row,
  378:                 ...(isSel ? ui.rowSelected : null),
  379:                 ...(dim ? ui.rowDim : null),
  380:                 ...(canSelect ? ui.rowClickable : null),
  381:                 cursor: disabled ? "not-allowed" : canSelect ? "pointer" : "default",
  382:                 opacity: disabled ? 0.72 : undefined,
  383:                 filter: disabled ? "grayscale(0.18)" : undefined,
  384:               }}
  385:               onClick={canSelect ? () => handleSelectGrupo(r.grupo) : undefined}
  386:               title={
  387:                 disabled
  388:                   ? "Bloqueado no modo demonstração"
  389:                   : canSelect
  390:                   ? "Clique para selecionar (toggle)"
  391:                   : undefined
  392:               }
  393:             >
  394:               <div style={ui.cellImg}>
  395:                 <div
  396:                   style={{
  397:                     ...ui.imgFrame,
  398:                     ...(isSel ? ui.imgFrameSelected : null),
  399:                     ...(dim ? ui.imgFrameDim : null),
  400:                   }}
  401:                 >
  402:                   {initialSrc ? (
  403:                     <img
  404:                       src={initialSrc}
  405:                       alt={r.animalLabel || `Grupo ${formatGrupo2(r.grupo)}`}
  406:                       style={{ ...ui.img, ...(dim ? ui.imgDim : null) }}
  407:                       loading="lazy"
  408:                       data-idx="0"
  409:                       onError={(e) => {
  410:                         const imgEl = e.currentTarget;
  411:                         const list = Array.isArray(r.imgCandidates) ? r.imgCandidates : [];
  412:                         const cur = Number(imgEl.dataset.idx || "0");
  413:                         const next = cur + 1;
  414: 
  415:                         if (list[next]) {
  416:                           imgEl.dataset.idx = String(next);
  417:                           imgEl.src = list[next];
  418:                           imgEl.style.visibility = "visible";
  419:                           return;
  420:                         }
  421: 
  422:                         imgEl.style.visibility = "hidden";
  423:                         const wrap = imgEl.parentElement;
  424:                         if (wrap && wrap instanceof HTMLElement) {
  425:                           wrap.dataset.noimg = "1";
  426:                         }
  427:                       }}
  428:                       onLoad={(e) => {
  429:                         const imgEl = e.currentTarget;
  430:                         imgEl.style.visibility = "visible";
  431:                         const wrap = imgEl.parentElement;
  432:                         if (wrap && wrap instanceof HTMLElement) {
  433:                           wrap.dataset.noimg = "0";
  434:                         }
  435:                       }}
  436:                     />
  437:                   ) : null}
  438: 
  439:                   <div style={ui.imgPlaceholder} aria-hidden="true">
  440:                     {formatGrupo2(r.grupo)}
  441:                   </div>
  442:                 </div>
  443:               </div>
  444: 
  445:               <div
  446:                 style={{
  447:                   ...ui.td,

  449:                   ...ui.numCell,
  450:                   ...(dim ? ui.tdDim : null),

    1: // src/pages/Dashboard/components/SearchResultsTable.jsx
    2: import React, { useMemo, useState, useCallback, useEffect } from "react";
    3: import {
    4:   getImgFromGrupo as getImgFromGrupoFn,
    5:   getAnimalLabel as getAnimalLabelFn,
    6: } from "../../../constants/bichoMap";
    7: 
    8: function pad2(n) {
    9:   return String(n).padStart(2, "0");
   10: }
   11: 
   12: function ymdToBR(ymd) {
   13:   const m = String(ymd || "")
   14:     .trim()
   15:     .match(/^(\d{4})-(\d{2})-(\d{2})$/);
   16:   if (!m) return "";
   17:   return `${m[3]}/${m[2]}/${m[1]}`;
   18: }
   19: 
   20: function safeStr(v) {
   21:   return String(v ?? "").trim();
   22: }
   23: 
   24: function isYMD(s) {
   25:   return /^\d{4}-\d{2}-\d{2}$/.test(String(s || "").trim());
   26: }
   27: 
   28: /* =========================
   29:    Hora: padronizar para bucket "09h", "11h"...
   30: ========================= */
   31: function hourBucketFromAny(value) {
   32:   const s0 = safeStr(value);
   33:   if (!s0) return "";
   34: 
   35:   const s = s0.replace(/\s+/g, "");
   36: 
   37:   // "11h", "11hs", "11hr"...
   38:   const mh = s.match(/^(\d{1,2})(?:h|hs|hr|hrs)$/i);
   39:   if (mh) return `${pad2(mh[1])}h`;
   40: 
   41:   // "11:10" ou "11:00" ou "11:10:00"
   42:   const mISO = s.match(/^(\d{1,2}):(\d{2})(?::(\d{2}))?$/);
   43:   if (mISO) return `${pad2(mISO[1])}h`;
   44: 
   45:   // "11"
   46:   const m2 = s.match(/^(\d{1,2})$/);
   47:   if (m2) return `${pad2(m2[1])}h`;
   48: 
   49:   return s0; // fallback (não deveria)
   50: }
   51: 
   52: /**
   53:  * ✅ Base URL para assets do public/
   54:  * - CRA: process.env.PUBLIC_URL
   55:  * - fallback: "" (raiz)
   56:  */
   57: function publicBase() {
   58:   const b = String(process.env.PUBLIC_URL || "").trim();
   59:   return b && b !== "/" ? b : "";
   60: }
   61: 
   62: /**
   63:  * ✅ Normaliza caminhos de imagem para o React (public/)
   64:  */
   65: function normalizeImgSrc(src) {
   66:   const s = safeStr(src);
   67:   if (!s) return "";
   68: 
   69:   if (/^https?:\/\//i.test(s)) return s;
   70: 
   71:   const base = publicBase();
   72: 
   73:   if (s.startsWith("/")) return `${base}${s}`;
   74: 
   75:   if (s.startsWith("public/")) return `${base}/${s.slice("public/".length)}`;
   76:   if (s.startsWith("img/")) return `${base}/${s}`;
   77: 
   78:   return `${base}/${s}`;
   79: }
   80: 
   81: function swapSizeInPath(path, fromSize, toSize) {
   82:   // troca "_96." por "_128." etc
   83:   return path.replace(new RegExp(`_${fromSize}(?=\\.)`, "g"), `_${toSize}`);
   84: }
   85: 
   86: function ensureSizeVariants(baseNoQuery) {
   87:   const out = [];
   88: 
   89:   // se já tem _64/_96/_128 no nome, tenta os outros
   90:   const has64 = /_64\./.test(baseNoQuery);
   91:   const has96 = /_96\./.test(baseNoQuery);
   92:   const has128 = /_128\./.test(baseNoQuery);
   93: 
   94:   if (has96) {
   95:     out.push(swapSizeInPath(baseNoQuery, 96, 128));
   96:     out.push(swapSizeInPath(baseNoQuery, 96, 64));
   97:   }
   98:   if (has128) {
   99:     out.push(swapSizeInPath(baseNoQuery, 128, 96));
  100:     out.push(swapSizeInPath(baseNoQuery, 128, 64));
  101:   }
  102:   if (has64) {
  103:     out.push(swapSizeInPath(baseNoQuery, 64, 96));
  104:     out.push(swapSizeInPath(baseNoQuery, 64, 128));
  105:   }
  106: 
  107:   // se NÃO tiver size no nome, tenta inserir antes da extensão
  108:   if (!has64 && !has96 && !has128) {
  109:     const m = baseNoQuery.match(/^(.*)\.(png|PNG|jpg|jpeg)$/);
  110:     if (m) {
  111:       const stem = m[1];
  112:       const ext = m[2];
  113:       out.push(`${stem}_96.${ext}`);
  114:       out.push(`${stem}_128.${ext}`);
  115:       out.push(`${stem}_64.${ext}`);
  116:     }
  117:   }
  118: 
  119:   return out;
  120: }
  121: 
  122: function makeImgVariants(src) {
  123:   const current = normalizeImgSrc(src);
  124:   if (!current) return [];
  125: 
  126:   const baseNoQuery = current.split("?")[0];
  127:   const out = [];
  128: 
  129:   // principal
  130:   out.push(baseNoQuery);
  131: 
  132:   // variações de size (muito comum o erro ser pedir _96 e existir _128)
  133:   out.push(...ensureSizeVariants(baseNoQuery));
  134: 
  135:   // alterna case/extensão
  136:   if (baseNoQuery.match(/\.png$/)) out.push(baseNoQuery.replace(/\.png$/, ".PNG"));
  137:   if (baseNoQuery.match(/\.PNG$/)) out.push(baseNoQuery.replace(/\.PNG$/, ".png"));
  138: 
  139:   // tenta jpg/jpeg
  140:   out.push(baseNoQuery.replace(/\.(png|PNG)$/i, ".jpg"));
  141:   out.push(baseNoQuery.replace(/\.(png|PNG|jpg)$/i, ".jpeg"));
  142: 
  143:   // também troca extensão nas variantes de size (caso stem exista só em jpg etc)
  144:   const extra = [];
  145:   for (const p of out) {
  146:     extra.push(p.replace(/\.(png|PNG)$/i, ".jpg"));
  147:     extra.push(p.replace(/\.(png|PNG|jpg)$/i, ".jpeg"));
  148:     if (p.match(/\.png$/)) extra.push(p.replace(/\.png$/, ".PNG"));
  149:     if (p.match(/\.PNG$/)) extra.push(p.replace(/\.PNG$/, ".png"));
  150:   }
  151: 
  152:   return Array.from(new Set([...out, ...extra].filter(Boolean)));
  153: }
  154: 
  155: function RowImg({ variants, alt, fallbackText }) {
  156:   const [failed, setFailed] = useState(false);
  157: 
  158:   const primary = variants?.[0] || "";
  159: 
  160:   // ✅ quando variants muda (nova linha/grupo), reseta o fallback
  161:   useEffect(() => {
  162:     setFailed(false);
  163:   }, [primary]); // muda o "principal" => reseta
  164: 
  165:   if (!Array.isArray(variants) || !variants.length || failed) {
  166:     return <div className="imgFallback">{fallbackText || "—"}</div>;
  167:   }
  168: 
  169:   return (
  170:     <img
  171:       key={variants[0]} // ✅ força reset do <img> ao trocar a imagem base
  172:       src={variants[0]}
  173:       alt={alt}
  174:       loading="lazy"
  175:       data-try="0"
  176:       onError={(e) => {
  177:         const imgEl = e.currentTarget;
  178:         const i = Number(imgEl.dataset.try || "0");
  179:         const next = variants[i + 1];
  180: 
  181:         if (next) {
  182:           imgEl.dataset.try = String(i + 1);
  183:           imgEl.src = next;
  184:         } else {
  185:           setFailed(true);
  186:         }
  187:       }}
  188:     />
  189:   );
  190: }
  191: /* =========================
  192:    Normalização de row (compat)
  193: ========================= */
  194: 
  195: function pickGrupo(r) {
  196:   const g =
  197:     r?.grupo ??
  198:     r?.grupo2 ??
  199:     r?.group ??
  200:     r?.group2 ??
  201:     r?.animal_grupo ??
  202:     r?.animalGrupo ??
  203:     null;
  204: 
  205:   const n = Number(String(g ?? "").replace(/^0+/, "") || "");
  206:   return Number.isFinite(n) ? n : null;
  207: }
  208: 
  209: function pickPos(r) {
  210:   const p = r?.position ?? r?.pos ?? r?.posicao ?? r?.colocacao ?? null;
  211:   const n = Number(p);
  212:   return Number.isFinite(n) ? n : null;
  213: }
  214: 
  215: function pickHour(r) {
  216:   return (
  217:     safeStr(r?.close_hour) ||
  218:     safeStr(r?.closeHour) ||
  219:     safeStr(r?.hour) ||
  220:     safeStr(r?.hora) ||
  221:     ""
  222:   );
  223: }
  224: 
  225: function pickYmd(r) {
  226:   const y =
  227:     safeStr(r?.ymd) ||
  228:     safeStr(r?.dateYmd) ||
  229:     safeStr(r?.date) ||
  230:     safeStr(r?.data) ||
  231:     "";
  232:   return isYMD(y) ? y : "";
  233: }
  234: 
  235: function pickMilhar4(r) {
  236:   const v =
  237:     r?.milhar4 ??
  238:     r?.milhar ??
  239:     r?.numero ??
  240:     r?.number ??
  241:     r?.num ??
  242:     r?.valor ??
  243:     r?.n ??
  244:     "";
  245:   const digits = safeStr(v).replace(/\D+/g, "");
  246:   if (!digits) return "";
  247:   const last4 = digits.slice(-4).padStart(4, "0");
  248:   return /^\d{4}$/.test(last4) ? last4 : "";
  249: }
  250: 
  251: function resolveAnimalLabelCompat(fn, grupo) {
  252:   if (typeof fn !== "function") return "";
  253:   const g = Number(grupo);
  254:   if (!Number.isFinite(g)) return "";
  255: 
  256:   try {
  257:     const a = fn({ grupo: g, animal: "" });
  258:     const s = safeStr(a);
  259:     if (s) return s;
  260:   } catch {}
  261: 
  262:   try {
  263:     const b = fn(g);
  264:     const s = safeStr(b);
  265:     if (s) return s;
  266:   } catch {}
  267: 
  268:   return "";
  269: }
  270: 
  271: function resolveImgCompat(fn, grupo) {
  272:   if (typeof fn !== "function") return "";
  273:   const g = Number(grupo);
  274:   if (!Number.isFinite(g)) return "";
  275: 
  276:   try {
  277:     const a = fn(g, 96);
  278:     const s = safeStr(a);
  279:     if (s) return s;
  280:   } catch {}
  281: 
  282:   try {
  283:     const b = fn(g);
  284:     const s = safeStr(b);
  285:     if (s) return s;
  286:   } catch {}
  287: 
  288:   return "";
  289: }
  290: 
  291: export default function SearchResultsTable({
  292:   rows,
  293:   data,
  294:   matches,
  295:   getAnimalLabel,
  296:   getImgFromGrupo,
  297: }) {
  298:   const safeRows = useMemo(() => {
  299:     const src = Array.isArray(rows)
  300:       ? rows
  301:       : Array.isArray(data)
  302:       ? data
  303:       : Array.isArray(matches)
  304:       ? matches
  305:       : [];
  306:     return src;
  307:   }, [rows, data, matches]);
  308: 
  309:   const resolveAnimalLabel = useCallback(
  310:     (grupo) => {
  311:       const fn = typeof getAnimalLabel === "function" ? getAnimalLabel : getAnimalLabelFn;
  312:       return resolveAnimalLabelCompat(fn, grupo);
  313:     },
  314:     [getAnimalLabel]
  315:   );
  316: 
  317:   const resolveImg = useCallback(
  318:     (grupo) => {
  319:       const fn = typeof getImgFromGrupo === "function" ? getImgFromGrupo : getImgFromGrupoFn;
  320:       return resolveImgCompat(fn, grupo);
  321:     },
  322:     [getImgFromGrupo]
  323:   );
  324: 
  325:   const normalized = useMemo(() => {
  326:     return safeRows.map((r, idx) => {
  327:       const grupo = pickGrupo(r);
  328:       const position = pickPos(r);
  329:       const ymd = pickYmd(r);
  330:       const close_hour_raw = pickHour(r);
  331:       const close_hour = hourBucketFromAny(close_hour_raw); // ✅ PADRÃO "09h"
  332: 
  333:       const milhar4 = safeStr(r?.milhar4) || pickMilhar4(r);
  334: 
  335:       return {
  336:         __idx: idx,
  337:         __raw: r,
  338:         ymd,
  339:         close_hour,
  340:         position,
  341:         grupo,
  342:         milhar4,
  343:       };
  344:     });
  345:   }, [safeRows]);
  346: 
  347:   return (
  348:     <div className="ppSearchTableWrap">
  349:       <style>{`
  350:         .ppSearchTableWrap{
  351:           border-radius:18px;
  352:           border:1px solid rgba(202,166,75,0.16);
  353:           background:rgba(0,0,0,0.35);
  354:           box-shadow:0 20px 60px rgba(0,0,0,0.35);
  355:           overflow:hidden;
  356:           flex:1 1 auto;
  357:           min-height:0;
  358:           min-width:0;
  359:           display:flex;
  360:           flex-direction:column;
  361:         }
  362: 
  363:         .ppSearchTableHead{
  364:           padding:10px 12px;
  365:           border-bottom:1px solid rgba(255,255,255,0.06);
  366:           font-size:10px;
  367:           color:rgba(233,233,233,0.68);
  368:           text-transform:uppercase;
  369:           letter-spacing:0.6px;
  370:           display:flex;
  371:           justify-content:space-between;
  372:           align-items:center;
  373:           flex:0 0 auto;
  374:           min-width:0;
  375:         }
  376: 
  377:         .ppSearchTableBody{
  378:           overflow:auto;
  379:           min-height:0;
  380:           padding:10px 10px 12px;
  381:           flex:1 1 auto;
  382:           min-width:0;
  383:         }
  384: 
  385:         .ppTable{
  386:           width:100%;
  387:           display:flex;
  388:           justify-content:center;
  389:           min-width:0;
  390:         }
  391: 
  392:         /* ✅ CRÍTICO: aqui está o fix do "corte"
  393:            - container permite scroll horizontal quando a largura não couber
  394:            - tabela tem min-width (não encolhe até cortar colunas)
  395:         */
  396:         .ppTableInner{
  397:           width: 100%;
  398:           max-width: 980px;      /* ✅ cresce quando tem espaço */
  399:           min-width: 0;
  400:           border-radius:14px;
  401:           overflow:auto;          /* ✅ scroll horizontal/vertical interno do "inner" */
  402:           border:1px solid rgba(255,255,255,0.06);
  403:           background:rgba(0,0,0,0.18);
  404:         }
  405:

  409:           border-collapse:collapse;

  484:         }
  485:       `}</style>
  486: 
  487:       <div className="ppSearchTableHead">

  489:         <div>
  490:           Total: <span className="gold">{normalized.length}</span>
  491:         </div>
  492:       </div>
  493: 
  494:       <div className="ppSearchTableBody">
  495:         <div className="ppTable">
  496:           <div className="ppTableInner">
  497:             <table>
  498:               <thead>
  499:                 <tr>
  500:                   <th style={{ width: 110 }}>Data</th>
  501:                   <th style={{ width: 90 }}>Horário</th>
  502:                   <th style={{ width: 90 }}>Posição</th>
  503:                   <th style={{ width: 120 }}>Número</th>
  504:                   <th style={{ width: 140 }}>Grupo</th>
  505:                   <th style={{ width: 90 }}>Imagem</th>
  506:                   <th style={{ width: 180 }}>Bicho</th>
  507:                 </tr>
  508:               </thead>
  509: 
  510:               <tbody>
  511:                 {normalized.map((r) => {
  512:                   const g = r.grupo;
  513:                   const hasGrupo = Number.isFinite(g) && g >= 1 && g <= 25;
  514: 
  515:                   const animal = hasGrupo ? resolveAnimalLabel(g) : "";
  516:                   const img0raw = hasGrupo ? resolveImg(g) : "";
  517:                   const variants = hasGrupo ? makeImgVariants(img0raw) : [];
  518: 
  519:                   const key = `${r.ymd || "x"}-${r.close_hour || "h"}-${r.position ?? "p"}-${r.milhar4 || "n"}-${r.__idx}`;
  520:

  528:

  531:                           className="imgFrame"

  536:                               ? `Grupo ${pad2(g)}`
  537:                               : ""
  538:                           }
  539:                         >
  540:                           <RowImg
  541:                             variants={variants}
  542:                             alt={animal || (hasGrupo ? `Grupo ${pad2(g)}` : "—")}
  543:                             fallbackText={hasGrupo ? `G${pad2(g)}` : "—"}
  544:                           />
  545:                         </div>
  546:                       </td>
  547: 
  548:                       <td className="bicho" title={animal || ""}>
  549:                         {animal || "—"}
  550:                       </td>
  551:                     </tr>
  552:                   );
  553:                 })}
  554: 
  555:                 {!normalized.length ? (
  556:                   <tr>
  557:                     <td
  558:                       colSpan={7}
  559:                       style={{
  560:                         padding: 14,
  561:                         textAlign: "center",
  562:                         color: "rgba(233,233,233,0.70)",
  563:                       }}
  564:                     >
  565:                       Nenhum resultado (digite 2, 3 ou 4 dígitos).
  566:                     </td>
  567:                   </tr>
  568:                 ) : null}
  569:               </tbody>
  570:             </table>
  571:           </div>
  572:         </div>
  573:       </div>
  574:     </div>
  575:   );
  576: }
